

The university presents its academic environment as a community of scholars, students, and alumni focused on learning, innovation, research, and social impact. Its stated approach combines academic excellence with practical teaching, industry interaction, international cooperation, entrepreneurship, communication skills, and opportunities for professional development.


The university website lists programmes including B.Tech CSE (AIML), B.Tech CSE (AIDS), B.Tech CSE (Cyber Security), B.Tech CSE, Bachelor of Business Administration, Bachelor of Commerce, Master of Business Administration, and M.Tech CSE Interdisciplinary Studies.


Admission Process

Admission to the B.Tech program at Sanjivani University is conducted
through the centralized admission process governed by the Directorate
of Technical Education, Maharashtra. Candidates must have passed HSC
with Physics, Chemistry, and Mathematics as compulsory subjects.

Fee Structure

The annual tuition fee for the B.Tech program is revised each academic
year as per the Fee Regulating Authority notification and is payable in
two installments, one at the start of each semester.


Examination Rules

Students must maintain a minimum of 75% attendance in each subject to be
eligible to sit for the semester end examinations. Any student falling
below this threshold must apply for condonation through the department
office at least two weeks before the examination schedule is published.

Backlog Policy

Students carrying backlog subjects from previous semesters are permitted
to appear for a maximum of two backlog papers alongside their current
semester examinations, subject to timetable clashes being resolved by
the examination cell.

Revaluation

Students who wish to apply for revaluation of their answer sheets must
submit a written application to the examination department within seven
days of the result declaration, along with the prescribed revaluation fee.


The university website reports that Fortinet partnered with Sanjivani University to establish a Fortinet Centre of Excellence on August 28, 2024. It also reports the inauguration of an IBM Centre of Excellence on January 23, 2025. These initiatives are presented as supporting cybersecurity education, industry-relevant training, real-world project experience, technological skills, industry readiness, and innovation.


Library Timings

The central library at Sanjivani University is open from 8:00 AM to
9:00 PM on all working days, Monday through Saturday. On Sundays and
public holidays, the library remains open from 10:00 AM to 4:00 PM for
reading room access only, with book issue and return services suspended.

Book Borrowing Policy

Undergraduate students may borrow up to three books at a time for a
period of fourteen days. Postgraduate students and research scholars
may borrow up to five books for a period of thirty days.

Sanjivani University is an institution of higher education established on the 40th anniversary of the Sanjivani Rural Education Society. The university emphasizes academic rigor, innovation, research, global cooperation, industry interaction, and student development. Its academic offerings include Engineering and Technology, Commerce and Management, Social Science and Humanities, School of Sciences, School of Pharmaceutical Science, School of Foreign Languages, School of Interdisciplinary Studies, undergraduate and postgraduate programmes, and global leadership programmes.

24UAMMD401 Research Methodology L T P
3 0 0
Category MCM
Type Theory
Prerequisites Nill

After completing the course, students are expected to achieve the following industry-relevant outcomes.
BTL
CO Number Course Outcome
COPMD401.1 Understand the foundations of research including theory, scientific method, variables, and hypothesis formulation for a given research problem. 2
COMD401.2 Apply appropriate research designs (exploratory, descriptive, experimental, qualitative, quantitative) for different research objectives. 3
COMD401.3 Apply suitable measurement techniques (validity, reliability, levels of measurement) and sampling methods to a research study. 3
COMD401.4 Analyze statistical and computational methods (descriptive statistics, hypothesis testing, regression) using Python/R for data-driven research analysis. 4
COMD401.5 Analyze the use of academic databases, reference management software (Zotero/Mendeley), formatting tools (LaTeX/MS Office), and plagiarism detection tools for effective research reporting 4

CO-PO /PSO Mapping
Mapping of Course Outcomes to Program Outcomes (POs) & Program Specific Outcomes (PSOs):

Course Contents
UNIT I Foundations of Research 9 hrs.
Foundations of Research: Meaning, Objectives, Motivation, Utility. Concept of theory, empiricism, deductive and inductive theory. Characteristics of scientific method – Understanding the language of research – Concept, Construct, Definition, Variable. Research Process Problem Identification & Formulation – Research Question – Investigation Question – Measurement Issues – Hypothesis – Qualities of a good Hypothesis –Null Hypothesis & Alternative Hypothesis. Hypothesis Testing – Logic & Importance, Introduction to Systematic Literature Review (SLR), Literature review techniques (PRISMA, Scopus search),
UNIT II Research Design 9 hrs.
Research Design: Concept and Importance in Research – Features of a good research design – Exploratory Research Design – concept, types and uses, Descriptive Research Designs – concept, types and uses. Experimental Design: Concept of Independent & Dependent variables. Qualitative and Quantitative Research: Qualitative research – Quantitative research – Concept of measurement, causality, generalization, replication. Merging the two approaches, Design thinking in research, AI experiment design (train-test-validation split), Reproducibility concepts.
UNIT III Measurement 9 hrs.
Concept of measurement– what is measured? Problems in measurement in research – Validity and Reliability. Levels of measurement – Nominal, Ordinal, Interval, Ratio. Sampling: Concepts of Statistical Population, Sample, Sampling Frame, Sampling Error, Sample Size, Non Response. Characteristics of a good sample. Probability Sample – Simple Random Sample, Systematic Sample, Stratified Random Sample & Multi-stage sampling. Determining size of the sample – Practical considerations in sampling and sample size, Feature engineering basics, Data collection ethics (AI datasets), Bias in datasets.
UNIT IV Data Analysis 9 hrs.
Data Preparation – Univariate analysis (frequency tables, bar charts, pie charts, percentages), Bivariate analysis – Cross tabulations and Chi-square test including testing hypothesis of association. Descriptive Statistics, Correlation & Regression, Hypothesis Testing- t-test ANOVA, Interpretation of Data and Paper Writing – Layout of a Research Paper, Journals in Computer Science, Impact factor of Journals, When and where to publish ? Ethical issues related to publishing, Plagiarism and Self-Plagiarism, Introduction to Machine Learning evaluation metrics Accuracy, Precision, Recall, F1-score (Python or R)
UNIT V AI for Research and Emerging Research Tools 9 hrs.
AI Tools for Research: Prompt Engineering, Prompt using in research , ChatGPT, Gemini, Elicit, Scite.ai, Research Rabbit, Semantic Scholar, Consensus AI, Connected Papers, GitHub Copilot, Perplexity AI, AI-based Coding Assistants, Research Automation Tools, Prompt Engineering for Research, Ethical Use of AI in Academic Research, Future Trends in AI-assisted Research.
Total Hours: 45 hrs

Learning Resources
Text Book:
1. Donald Cooper & Pamela Schindler, (2008), Business Research Methods, 9th Edition, Tata McGraw-Hill Education (TMGH), ISBN: 9780070141184.
2. Alan Bryman & Emma Bell, (2015), Business Research Methods, Oxford University Press, ISBN: 9780199668649.
3. C.R. Kothari, (2004), Research Methodology: Methods and Techniques, 2nd Revised Edition, New Age International Publishers, ISBN: 9788122415223.
Reference Book:

Suggested Self-Learning (SL):
Assignment, Seminar Topics, Visit, Complex Engineering Problems and Micro Projects- Questions/ Problems/ Numerical/ Exercises to be provided by the course teacher in line with the targeted COs. (Co Wise)
SL Particulars Targeted CO No of Hrs Topics Outcome
1 Seminar CO4, CO5 3 Ethical issues in publishing, plagiarism, self-plagiarism, and impact factor of journals in Computer Science
Students will be able to analyze ethical issues in academic publishing and identify appropriate journals for research publication
2 Assignments CO 1,2 & 3 6 1. Formulation of research question & hypothesis
2. Designing a research design for a given problem
3. Sampling technique selection & sample size determination Students will be able to understand research foundations, apply appropriate research designs, and apply suitable sampling methods for a given research problem
3 Self- Learning Topics CO 1 & 5 4 Concept of theory vs empiricism, deductive vs inductive theory, use of encyclopedias, research guides, and handbooks Students will be able to understand theoretical foundations of research and analyze the use of academic reference resources
4 Complex Engineering Problems CO 3 & 4 5 Problems in measurement (validity/reliability issues), interpretation of Chi-square test for association, handling non-response in sampling Students will be able to apply measurement techniques to solve complex research problems and analyze data using Chi-square test
5 Micro Projects: CO 2,3 & 4 8 Conduct a mini research study including: problem identification, research design selection, measurement scale development, data collection (n=30), and basic bivariate analysis with interpretation Students will be able to apply research designs and measurement techniques, and analyze data to interpret research findings effectively
6 Visits CO 5 2 Visit to a library or research center to explore academic databases (IEEE, ACM, Scopus) and reference management software (Zotero/Mendeley) demonstration Students will be able to analyze the use of academic databases and reference management tools for effective research reporting

APPLY

Fill in the application form with academic and personal info.

Review carefully before submission.

COMPLETE APPLICATION FORM

Make the application fee payment securely online.

This ensures your form is considered for processing.

DOCUMENT VERIFICATION

Get your submitted documents verified by the admissions team.Pay the token fee to confirm your interest in the program.

FINAL ADMISSION

Receive official confirmation of your admission.

Your seat is now secured with the institution.

INSTRUCTIONS

Read each step carefully before moving to the next.

Ensure all details entered are accurate and complete.

Keep scanned copies of required documents ready.

Complete fee payments promptly to avoid delays.

Regularly check your registered email/SMS for updates.

Reach out to the admissions team if you face any issues.

Remember: Admission is confirmed only after token fee payment and official confirmation.

Life at Sanjivani

“Sanjivani University, situated in the peaceful surroundings of Taluka Kopargaon, invites you. Our campus provides a calm place for academic pursuits because of its beautiful surroundings and a tremendous amount of nature. Situated in the glory of nature, Sanjivani University maintains a balance between accessibility and peace. Here, in the center of campus activity, students experience a dynamic academic environment bolstered by state-of-the-art facilities and a supportive community. Every student at Sanjivani University is guaranteed an enriching and gratifying experience, regardless of whether they choose to take up demanding academic endeavors or grasp the many chances for personal development.”

The Legacy of

Learning at Sanjivani University

Sanjivani’s legacy is defined by excellence, innovation, and global cooperation. Prioritizing academic rigor and research, it fosters a culture of continuous learning. By forging partnerships with renowned universities worldwide, Sanjivani enhances the academic journey for students and facilitates cutting-edge research opportunities for faculty. Yet, its most profound impact lies in its community of scholars, students, and alumni united in driving positive change. Together, they propel Sanjivani as a beacon of knowledge, innovation, and social impact, ensuring a lasting legacy for generations to come.

ABOUT SANJIVANI UNIVERSITY

About Sanjivani University

About Sanjivani University

About Sanjivani University

Sanjivani University, Kopargaon, is a premier institution of higher education committed to excellence in teaching, research, and innovation.

Established under the visionary leadership of the Sanjivani Group of Institutes, the University was founded with the objective of creating a transformative learning environment that nurtures intellectual growth, professional competence, and social responsibility.

Offering a comprehensive range of undergraduate, postgraduate, and doctoral programs across Engineering & Technology, Management, Commerce, Sciences, Pharmacy, and interdisciplinary domains, Sanjivani University ensures academic depth combined with practical relevance.

The University follows a learner-centric and outcome-based education model aligned with the National Education Policy (NEP) 2020, emphasizing experiential learning, skill development, entrepreneurship, research orientation, and industry readiness.

Through strong industry partnerships, incubation and innovation centers, internships, global collaborations, and hands-on projects, students gain real-world exposure and global perspectives.

Rooted in a rich legacy of ethical values and community engagement, and supported by experienced faculty, state-of-the-art infrastructure, and a digitally enabled campus, Sanjivani University strives to develop future-ready professionals and responsible global citizens who contribute meaningfully to nation-building, sustainable development, and societal progress.

ADMISSION CANCELLATION & REFUND POLICY

Home

Admission Cancellation & Refund Policy

Admission Cancellation & Refund Policy

Sanjivani University adheres to a transparent and student-friendly policy for admission cancellation and fee refund, in accordance with the guidelines issued by the University Grants Commission (UGC) and relevant statutory authorities.

1. Procedure for Admission Cancellation

A student seeking to cancel their admission must submit a written request (physically or through the admission portal) along with a copy of the admission receipt and valid ID proof.

The application must be submitted to the Registrar's Office or through the designated online channel.

Cancellation will be considered effective from the date the formal request is received by the university.

2. Refund Policy Structure

The refund of fees shall be applicable only for students who have paid the full or part admission fees and submitted a valid cancellation request:

Time of Cancellation Refund Amount

Before commencement of academic session 100% refund of fees (after deducting ₹1,000)

Within 15 days after the commencement of academic session 90% refund of total fees

Between 16 to 30 days after commencement 50% refund of total fees

After 30 days from commencement of academic session No refund applicable

Note: In all cases, the Caution Money (if applicable) will be refunded fully after clearance of dues, even if the tuition fee is non-refundable.

Sanjivani University, Kopargaon,

(Maharashtra), 423601.

AWARDS & RECOGNITION

Home

Awards & Recognition

Awards & Recognition

TECHNOLOGY & AI EDUCATION RANKINGS 2025

Certificate of Recognition - Institution of Transformative Learning -2025

Sanjivani University, Maharashtra, has been honoured with a Certificate of Recognition by Chronicles of India for its outstanding commitment to excellence in Technology and Artificial Intelligence education. The University has been ranked in Category A2 and recognized as an Institution of Transformative Learning under the Technology & AI Education Rankings 2025.

This prestigious recognition reflects Sanjivani University's continued efforts in advancing technology-driven and AI-focused education, fostering a culture of innovation and future-ready competencies, promoting ethical contributions in research and technology, strengthening teaching–learning pedagogy and entrepreneurship, and driving meaningful societal progress through education.

Awarded by: Chronicles of India

Category: A2 – Institution of Transformative Learning

Issued On: 21 February 2025

Certificate ID: 25TECH-AI-A2-44

STAR EDUCATION AWARD 2024

Best Emerging University in Rural - Maharashtra

Sanjivani University has been honoured with the Star Education Award 2024 with deepest appreciation in the category of Best Emerging University in Rural - Maharashtra. This prestigious recognition was presented by AMIT KOLHE during the award ceremony held on 24th, 26th, 27th November 2024 at BMC MARDA, Grounds, Mumbai.

Awarded by: Star Education Award

Category: Best Emerging University in Rural - Maharashtra

Date: 24th-27th November 2024

MHW RANKINGS

Recognition of Engagement in Mental Health & Wellbeing Assessment

This certifies that Sanjivani University participated in the MHW Rankings 2025-26 by R. World Institutional Ranking, and has been ranked in the Diamond+ (Advanced) Band - Distinguished Institutions in Campus Wellbeing, for demonstrated excellence in promoting mental health and wellbeing for students, faculty, and staff.

Awarded by: R World Institutional RANKING

Category: MHW RANKINGS

Institution of Transformative Learning

CERTIFICATE OF RECOGNITION

Sanjivani University has been recognized for its outstanding contributions to higher education. This award celebrates the university's dedication to academic excellence, innovative teaching methodologies, and commitment to student development. The recognition reflects the institution's continuous efforts to provide quality education and create future-ready graduates.

Awarded by:Chronicles of India

Institution: Sanjivani University, Maharashtra

Recognition: Excellence in Higher Education

Sanjivani University, Kopargaon,

Near Shirdi, Ahilyanagar

(Maharashtra), 423601.

CAREERS

Careers

Careers

Join Sanjivani University – Build Your Career with Purpose

Sanjivani University welcomes outcome-oriented professionals who are passionate about contributing to academic excellence and institutional growth across diverse domains.

If you are eligible and interested in becoming a part of our dynamic team, kindly share your detailed CV at joinus@sanjivani.org.in.

(Please mention “Application for the – <Department/School>” in the subject line.)

Career opportunities at Sanjivani University are regularly offered across various schools, departments, and administrative units, with recruitment managed by the Human Resources Department throughout the year.

Sanjivani University takes pride in fostering an inclusive work environment and offers competitive benefits, contributing meaningfully to the upliftment of all sections of society.

Join the Sanjivani Family!

We are always looking for talented, passionate, and dedicated individuals to be a part of Sanjivani University and Sanjivani Group of Institutes.

Submit your application through the form below and explore exciting career opportunities with us.

Link - https://forms.gle/L6DZmAQbFiwmMfDT7

CHAIRMAN’S MESSAGE

Home

Chairman’s Message

Chairman’s Message

Hon'ble Shri. Nitinrao Shankarrao Kolhe

Chairman, SRES

In the pursuit of excellence in education and global collaboration, Sanjivani University, under the leadership of its dedicated chairman, is committed to bringing quality international education to its students’ doorsteps. Through strategic partnerships with leading institutions worldwide, the university offers diverse programs and opportunities for students to engage with international faculty, exchange ideas with peers from different cultures, and gain valuable insights into global issues.

Beyond the classroom, Sanjivani University provides state-of-the-art facilities and hands-on learning experiences to ensure holistic development and prepare students for success in today's competitive landscape. Embracing a vision of inclusivity and empowerment, the university fosters an environment where learning knows no boundaries and where students from diverse backgrounds can thrive.

With a focus on equipping students with the knowledge, skills, and mindset needed to excel in their chosen fields, Sanjivani University is dedicated to building a brighter future for its community and the world at large.

Warm regards,

Hon'ble Shri. Nitinrao Shankarrao Kolhe

Chairman, SRES

Sanjivani University, Kopargaon,

Near Shirdi, Ahilyanagar

(Maharashtra), 423601.

DIRECTOR SD&W DESK

Home

Director SD&W Desk

Director SD&W Desk

Prof. Chaitanya Kale

Director, Student Development & Welfare

Welcome to the Student Development & Welfare Cell of Sanjivani University.

At Sanjivani University, we are committed to the holistic development of our students by fostering academic excellence, personal growth, leadership, social responsibility, and overall well-being. The Student Development & Welfare Cell serves as a bridge between students and the university administration, ensuring a supportive, inclusive, and enriching campus environment.

Our office coordinates various student-centric initiatives, including leadership development programs, career guidance activities, counseling services, NSS activities, cultural and sports events, student grievance redressal, community engagement programs, and welfare schemes. We strive to create opportunities that empower students to discover their potential, develop essential life skills, and become responsible global citizens.

We believe that education extends beyond the classroom. Therefore, we encourage students to actively participate in co-curricular and extracurricular activities that enhance their personality, confidence, and professional competencies.

The Student Development & Welfare Cell remains dedicated to addressing student concerns, promoting student engagement, and nurturing a vibrant campus culture that supports the overall growth and success of every learner.

We welcome your suggestions, participation, and collaboration in our collective journey toward excellence.

Best Wishes,

Prof. Chaitanya Kale

Director

Student Development & Welfare

Sanjivani University

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {

const toggler = document.querySelector(".navbar-toggler");

const header = document.querySelector(".site-header");

if (!toggler || !header) return;

toggler.addEventListener("click", function() {

header.classList.toggle("menu-open");

});

});

const header = document.querySelector(".site-header");

window.addEventListener("scroll", () => {

if (window.scrollY > 30) {

header.classList.add("scrolled");

} else {

header.classList.remove("scrolled");

});

// Handle clicking on the search icon to show/hide the input

document.querySelector('.search-icon').addEventListener('click', function(event) {

const searchInput = document.querySelector('.search-input');

searchInput.style.display = 'block'; // Show the input field

event.stopPropagation(); // Prevent the click from propagating to the document

});

// Handle clicking outside the input to hide it

document.addEventListener('click', function(event) {

const searchInput = document.querySelector('.search-input');

const searchIcon = document.querySelector('.search-icon');

// Check if the click is outside the input or the icon

if (!searchInput.contains(event.target) && !searchIcon.contains(event.target)) {

searchInput.style.display = 'none'; // Hide the input field

});

// Prevent hiding the input when clicking inside it

document.querySelector('.search-input').addEventListener('click', function(event) {

event.stopPropagation(); // Prevents the document click event from firing

});

s.async = true;

document.body.appendChild(s);

ELIGIBILITY

Home

Eligibility

Eligibility

ELIGIBILITY CRITERIA - SANJIVANI UNIVERSITY

SN Name of Programme Eligibility

01 Bachelor of Technology

(B. Tech)

• 10+2 with Physics & Math + one subject from Chemistry/Biology/Biotech/CS/IT/etc.

• Minimum 45% (Open), 40% (Reserved/EWS/PWD Maharashtra) in above subjects

• Must have non-zero score in MHT-CET 2025 (PCM Group) or Sanjivani University Entrance Test 2025 or PERA or JEE

02 Bachelor of Technology

(B. Tech) (Direct Second Year)

• Passed 3-year or 2-year Diploma in Engineering/Technology with minimum 45% marks (Open), 40% (Reserved/EWS/PWD – Maharashtra)

• Diploma must be from an AICTE/State/Central Govt. approved institute in any branch

• Passed B.Sc. Degree from a UGC/AIU recognized university

• Minimum 45% marks (Open), 40% (Reserved/EWS/PWD – Maharashtra)

• Must have studied Mathematics at 10+2 level

03 Master of Technology

(M. Tech)

• Passed Bachelor's Degree in Engineering / Technology from AICTE/State/Central Govt. approved institute

• Minimum 50% marks (Open), 45% (Reserved/EWS/PWD – Maharashtra)

• Must have non-Zero Score in GATE or Sanjivani University Entrance Examination

04 Bachelor of Technology (Integrated-6 Years)

(B. Tech-Integrated)

• Passed 10th Std. with Science and Mathematics from a State Board/CBSE/ICSE etc.

• Minimum 45% marks (Open), 40% (Reserved category – Maharashtra only)

• Must have non-Zero score in Sanjivani University Entrance Examination

05 Bachelor of Computer Applications

(BCA)

• Passed 10+2 from ICSE/CBSE/State Board or equivalent

• Science or Commerce stream with preferably Mathematics as a subject

• Must have non-Zero Score in Sanjivani University Entrance Exam

06 Master of Computer Applications

(MCA)

• Passed any Bachelor's Degree (e.g. B.Sc/B.Com/BA/BCA/B.Tech/B. Voc, etc.)

• Minimum 50% marks (Open) and 45% (Reserved/EWS/PWD – Maharashtra)

• Non-zero score in MAH-MCA CET or Sanjivani University Entrance Examination is mandatory

07 Master of Business Administration

(MBA)

• Passed 3-year Bachelor's Degree from a UGC/AIU recognized university in any discipline

• Minimum 50% marks (Open), 45% (Reserved/EWS/PWD – Maharashtra)

• Must have non-zero score in MAH-MBA/MMS CET or Sanjivani University Entrance Examination or equivalent exam (e.g., CAT/CMAT/GMAT)

08 Bachelor of Business Administration

(BBA)

• Passed 10+2 from any stream (ICSE/CBSE/State Board or equivalent)

• Must have non zero score in MAH – BBA CET or Sanjivani University Entrance Examination

09 Bachelor of Commerce

(B.COM)

• Passed 10+2 or equivalent examination from ICSE/CBSE/State Board

10 Bachelor of Pharmacy

(B. PHARM)

• 10+2 with Physics & Chemistry + one subject from Mathematics/Biology/Biotech/CS/IT/etc.

• Minimum 45% (Open), 40% (Reserved/EWS/PWD Maharashtra) in above subjects

• Must have non-zero score in MHT-CET 2025 (PCB Group) or Sanjivani University Entrance Test 2025 or NEET

11 Bachelor of Science

(B. Sc)

• Passed 10+2 Science from ICSE/CBSE/State Board or equivalent

12 Master of Science

(M. Sc)

• Passed B.Sc. Degree in the relevant subject from a UGC-recognized university

• Minimum 50% marks (Open category), 45% (Reserved/EWS/PWD – Maharashtra only)

Upcoming Event

Sanjivani University's partnership with Erdos Research, a prestigious community incubated at ( Oxford city,UK)

From: 2026-04-21 To: 2026-05-30

"Sanjivani University's momentous partnership with Erdos Research, a prestigious community incubated at ( Oxford city,UK) marks a new era of academic excellence and innovation. This landmark MoU signing ceremony, graced by esteemed dignitaries, solidifies Sanjivani University's position as a hub for cutting-edge research and societal impact.

The collaboration with Erdos Research, inspired by the legendary mathematician Paul Erdos, embodies the spirit of collaborative inquiry and intellectual exploration. By harnessing the power of courses and capstone projects, Erdos Research aims to address pressing societal challenges and foster sustainable development solutions.

Annual Gathering-Spectra 2k26-Inaguration of Days Celebration

From: 0000-00-00 To: 0000-00-00

Location: Sanjivani University

Sanjivani University celebrated the Annual Social Gathering – Spectra 2K26 with great enthusiasm and vibrant spirit. The highlight of the celebration was Traditional Day, organized around the inspiring theme of the Ramayana.

The celebration began with the auspicious Shri Ram Rath Yatra, filled with divine chants and devotional fervor. Students actively participated in the rally, beautifully showcasing India’s rich cultural heritage and traditional values through their attire, performances, and presentations.

The entire campus resonated with devotion, unity, and cultural pride, making the event truly memorable.

Annual Gathering-Spectra 2k26-Sports-Kurukshetra

From: 0000-00-00 To: 0000-00-00

Location: Sanjivani University

Kurukshetra – Annual Sports Meet Inauguration

It was a proud and inspiring moment for me to be present at the inauguration of the Annual Sports Meet – Kurukshetra at Sanjivani University today.

The ceremony was graced by the presence of Dr. A. G. Thakur (Vice Chancellor), Prof. Amol Dhakane (Registrar), Prof. Chaitanya Kale (Director, Student Development & Welfare), along with all Deans, Directors, Heads of Departments, faculty members, and our energetic students.

Witnessing the enthusiasm, discipline, and team spirit of our students truly filled me with pride. Sports play a vital role in shaping character, leadership, resilience, and the ability to strive for excellence — values that we strongly believe in at Sanjivani.

Kurukshetra is not just a sports meet; it is a celebration of determination, unity, and the spirit of healthy competition.</

Global Partnerships

Our Worldwide Industry Alliances

Sanjivani University partners with global technology and business leaders to deliver international-standard education. These collaborations bring cutting-edge tools, global certifications, Centres of Excellence, multinational projects, and high-impact placements — all integrated into the academic experience.

Students gain exposure to real-world global workflows, cross-border innovation, and career opportunities in international environments — preparing them for leadership in a connected world economy.

Through these global partnerships, Sanjivani University is creating professionals who think and work beyond geographical boundaries. Students gain international certifications, exposure to multinational workflows, real global projects, and direct pathways to careers in leading tech & business ecosystems worldwide.

Tomorrow's innovation leaders are being shaped today — right here at Sanjivani.

HEALTH FACILITIES

Home

Health Facilities

Health Facilities

Sanjivani University provides several facilities to support Divyangjan (specially-abled) students. These include a wheelchair, ambulance, and battery-operated vehicle for easy movement within the campus.

The university also has an on-campus clinic that operates at specified times to provide basic medical care. An ambulance is available to transport patients to hospitals during medical emergencies. It is equipped with medical tools and staffed by trained personnel to provide care on the way to the hospital. The ambulance has flashing lights and sirens to ensure a quick and safe journey.

Sanjivani University, Kopargaon,

Near Shirdi, Ahilyanagar

(Maharashtra), 423601.

Sanjivani University Hosts "Zdravstvuyte Russia (Namaste Russia)" to Strengthen Global Academic Collaborations.

Sanjivani University has successfully organized "Zdravstvuyte Russia (Namaste Russia)", an international engagement program aimed at introducing students to academic, research, and career opportunities in the Russian Federation.

The university had the privilege of welcoming Mr. Matvai Fetisov (Vice Consul General of the Russian Federation), Mr. Eugene (Head of Administration, Consulate of the Russian Federation) and Mr. Shivendrasinh Chauhan (Strategic Lead – Political & Institutional Alliance, AP Globale) as the Chief Guests. The dignitaries were welcomed by Hon'ble Shri. Amitdada Kolhe, President, Sanjivani University, Dr. A.G. Thakur, Vice Chancellor, and Dr. R.S. Shendge, Head of Academic Governance.

During the session, Mr. Matvai Fetisov shared valuable insights into the strengthening strategic partnership between India and Russia, highlighting opportunities in higher education, research collaborations, industrial partnerships, innovation, and global employment. His address encouraged students to broaden their global perspective and explore international pathways for academic and professional growth.

The event reflected Sanjivani University's commitment to fostering meaningful international collaborations and providing students with global exposure through interactions with distinguished diplomats, industry leaders, and academic experts.

The university expresses its sincere gratitude to the esteemed guests for their inspiring presence and acknowledges the dedicated efforts of Dr. Sudhanshu Bhatt (Director – University Relations), Prof. Amol Dhakane (Registrar), Dr. Mahendra Gawali (Dean – SIRD) and the entire organizing team for making the event a grand success.

Sanjivani University Signs MoU with LinkedIn Learning | 15,000+ Courses for Students

Sanjivani University Signs MoU with LinkedIn Learning | 15,000+ Courses for Students

Sanjivani University has signed a strategic Memorandum of Understanding (MoU) with LinkedIn Learning, unlocking access to 15,000+ online courses for its students across emerging technologies and professional domains.

Through this collaboration, students will gain access to industry-relevant learning, globally recognized certifications, and opportunities to strengthen their professional profiles and employability.

The MoU was signed in the presence of Hon’ble President Shri. Amitdada Kolhe, Registrar Mr. Amol Dhakane, Dr. Vinod Malkar, Dean, School of Online Education & Distance Learning, and Dr. Kavitha Rani, Dean, School of Engineering & Technology. The LinkedIn Learning delegation included Mr. Amit Mukherjee, Mr. Shivprasad Longani, and Mr. Anup Menon.

As part of Sankalp 2026, LinkedIn Learning representatives also engaged with first-year students, highlighting the importance of professional networking, continuous upskilling, certifications, and building a strong LinkedIn presence from the beginning of their academic journey.

This collaboration reflects Sanjivani University’s commitment to digital transformation, lifelong learning, industry readiness, and globally benchmarked education—empowering students with the skills and confidence to thrive in a rapidly evolving world.

Smart Kopargaon Hackathon 2026

Smart Kopargaon Hackathon 2026

Smart Kopargaon Hackathon 2026 is Sanjivani University's flagship national-level innovation challenge, bringing together students from across the country to develop technology-driven solutions for real-world problems aligned with Smart Kopargaon initiatives.

Highlights:

* Three-stage hybrid hackathon (Online + Offline)

* Team of 4 members

* Around 50 real-world problem statements

* Evaluation by academic and industry experts

* Dedicated mentorship throughout the competition

* Prize Pool: ₹1,00,000

This is an opportunity to solve real-world challenges, strengthen your technical and problem-solving skills, collaborate with talented innovators, and compete for national recognition.

HOW TO APPLY

Home

How To Apply

How To Apply

Admission Process

Follow these carefully structured steps to complete your admission process. Each step is essential to ensure a smooth transition into our academic community.

STEP

Online Application

Visit our official website www.sanjivani.edu.in to begin your application process.

STEP

Complete Application Form

Fill out the online application form with accurate personal, academic, and contact information.

STEP

CET Registration

Register for Common Entrance Test (CET) if applicable to your chosen program of study.

STEP

Application Fee Payment

Submit the non-refundable application processing fee. ₹1,000/- Only

STEP

Entrance Examination

Appear for the entrance test on the scheduled date. Ensure you bring valid identification and the admit card.

STEP

Merit List Publication

Check the published merit list on the official website to view your ranking and eligibility status.

STEP

Provisional Offer Letter

Receive your provisional admission offer letter based on merit ranking. This will be sent to your registered email address.

STEP

Admission Confirmation

Confirm your admission by paying the first installment of the program fees within the stipulated deadline.

STEP

Document Verification

Submit all required original documents for verification along with attested photocopies as per the checklist provided.

STEP

Final Admission Letter

Receive your official admission confirmation letter after successful verification of all documents and fee payment.

STEP

Student Credentials

Collect your official student ID card and university uniform from the designated department.

STEP

Commencement of Classes

Attend the orientation program and begin your academic journey as per the academic calendar schedule.

Academic Calendar

INDUSTRY CONNECTIONS

Industry Connections

Industry Connections

Connecting Campus to Corporate World

Sanjivani University maintains active collaborations with top technology and industry leaders to deliver practical education, industry certifications, internships, live projects, expert sessions and strong placement support.

Through dedicated Centres of Excellence, joint training programs and direct industry engagement, students gain exposure to current technologies and work practices — leading to high-value placements (highest up to 32+ LPA) in reputed companies.

What Our Partnerships Deliver

Centres of Excellence

Dedicated infrastructure & labs (IBM – 2025, Fortinet – 2024, Zoho – 2024) for hands-on learning in AI, cybersecurity, cloud & business applications.

Global Certifications

Industry-recognized credentials in emerging technologies, digital skills, cybersecurity, fintech and software solutions.

Internships & Live Projects

Real company projects, summer internships and collaborative development work with industry mentors.

Industry Expert Sessions

Frequent guest lectures, technical workshops, seminars and career guidance from practicing professionals.

Powerful Placements

Direct campus recruitment, high salary offers and career opportunities in leading MNCs and startups.

Innovation Support

Incubation facilities, startup mentoring and innovation challenges aligned with current industry trends.

INDUSTRY-POWERED CURRICULUM

Home

Industry-Powered Curriculum

Industry-Powered Curriculum

Curriculum That Evolves With Industry

Traditional education often lags behind the pace of technological and business change. At Sanjivani University, we eliminate that gap entirely.

Our curriculum is a living, breathing system — actively shaped through continuous dialogue with global industry leaders. Industry experts sit on our academic boards, contribute to syllabus design, review learning outcomes every semester, and co-author case studies, projects, and assessment frameworks. This ensures that what you study today is exactly what the industry demands tomorrow.

The result? No “transition period” from college to career. Our graduates enter the workforce already fluent in the tools, processes, and mindsets used by top employers worldwide — giving them a measurable edge in placements, promotions, and long-term career growth.

How Industry Actively Builds Your Education

Continuous Curriculum Co-Design

Biannual reviews with industry advisory councils ensure course content reflects emerging trends — from GenAI workflows and zero-trust security to sustainable fintech and supply-chain digital twins. Obsolete topics are replaced within months, not years.

Centres of Excellence as Living Labs

Dedicated CoEs function as real corporate environments — equipped, maintained, and updated by partners. Students solve live tickets, configure production-grade systems, develop customer-facing apps, and participate in partner hackathons and innovation challenges.

Mandatory Industry Immersion

Every program includes 6–12 months of structured industry immersion — paid internships, apprenticeships, or project-based residencies. Many students receive pre-placement offers during these periods, turning education into direct career acceleration.

Embedded Global Certifications

Certifications are not optional add-ons — they are core credits. Students earn vendor-neutral and vendor-specific credentials (e.g., Fortinet NSE levels, IBM AI Engineering, Zoho CRM Expert) as part of regular coursework — boosting resumes from semester one.

Industry-Driven Capstone & Electives

Final-year capstone projects are proposed and mentored by partner companies. Students choose from a live menu of real business problems. Elective pools are refreshed annually based on partner skill forecasts — ensuring relevance for the next 3–5 years.

Industry Faculty & Corporate Mentorship

20–30% of teaching hours come from practicing professionals. Each student gets a dedicated industry mentor for career guidance, resume building, interview preparation, and project feedback — creating a personal pipeline to employment.

Measurable Outcomes & Competitive Edge

100%+ placement rate with average CTC significantly above university benchmarks

Multiple offers before graduation — many students receive job offers during final-year internships

Global readiness — alumni working in USA, Singapore, Germany, UAE, and top Indian MNCs within first year

Higher retention & growth — early promotions and faster career trajectories due to immediate productivity

Infrastructure

Infrastructure

Sanjivani University to Organize ‘Sanjivani Sankalp 2K26’ Direct 2nd Year Induction Program

Published On: 14 Aug 2026

ustry leaders, providing students with valuable insights into academic excellence, professional development, industry expectations, innovation, and career opportunities. The program will be graced by Hon. Mrs. Lavanya Gangaram, Sr. Product Associate, JPMorgan Chase, Hyderabad, as the Chief Guest, along with the esteemed presence of university leadership and dignitaries, including Dr. Kavitha Rani P., Dean – SET; Prof. Amol Dhakane, Registrar; Dr. A. G. Thakur, Vice Chancellor; Hon. Shri. Amit N. Kolhe, President; and Hon. Shri. Nitinrao S. Kolhe, Chairman, Sanjivani Rural Education Society. Event Details: Date: Monday, 17 August 2026 Time: 10:30 AM to 12:30 PM Venue: Seminar Hall (101), Sanjivani University, Kopargaon (Near Shirdi), Dist. Ahilyanagar – 423601 Through Sanjivani Sankalp 2K26, the university aims to help students begin their academic journey with confidence, purpose, and a strong foundation for future success.

Sanjivani University Hosts "Zdravstvuyte Russia (Namaste Russia)" to Strengthen Global Academic Collaborations.

Published On: 07 Aug 2026

Sanjivani University has successfully organized "Zdravstvuyte Russia (Namaste Russia)", an international engagement program aimed at introducing students to academic, research, and career opportunities in the Russian Federation. The university had the privilege of welcoming Mr. Matvai Fetisov (Vice Consul General of the Russian Federation), Mr. Eugene (Head of Administration, Consulate of the Russian Federation) and Mr. Shivendrasinh Chauhan (Strategic Lead – Political & Institutional Alliance, AP Globale) as the Chief Guests. The dignitaries were welcomed by Hon'ble Shri. Amitdada Kolhe, President, Sanjivani University, Dr. A.G. Thakur, Vice Chancellor, and Dr. R.S. Shendge, Head of Academic Governance. During the session, Mr. Matvai Fetisov shared valuable insights into the strengthening strategic partnership between India and Russia, highlighting opportunities in higher education, research collaborations, industrial partnerships, innovation, and global employment. His address encouraged students to broaden their global perspective and explore international pathways for academic and professional growth. The event reflected Sanjivani University's commitment to fostering meaningful international collaborations and providing students with global exposure through interactions with distinguished diplomats, industry leaders, and academic experts. The university expresses its sincere gratitude to the esteemed guests for their inspiring presence and acknowledges the dedicated efforts of Dr. Sudhanshu Bhatt (Director – University Relations), Prof. Amol Dhakane (Registrar), Dr. Mahendra Gawali (Dean – SIRD) and the entire organizing team for making the event a grand success.

Sanjivani University Signs MoU with LinkedIn Learning | 15,000+ Courses for Students

Published On: 06 Aug 2026

Sanjivani University Signs MoU with LinkedIn Learning | 15,000+ Courses for Students Sanjivani University has signed a strategic Memorandum of Understanding (MoU) with LinkedIn Learning, unlocking access to 15,000+ online courses for its students across emerging technologies and professional domains. Through this collaboration, students will gain access to industry-relevant learning, globally recognized certifications, and opportunities to strengthen their professional profiles and employability. The MoU was signed in the presence of Hon’ble President Shri. Amitdada Kolhe, Registrar Mr. Amol Dhakane, Dr. Vinod Malkar, Dean, School of Online Education & Distance Learning, and Dr. Kavitha Rani, Dean, School of Engineering & Technology. The LinkedIn Learning delegation included Mr. Amit Mukherjee, Mr. Shivprasad Longani, and Mr. Anup Menon. As part of Sankalp 2026, LinkedIn Learning representatives also engaged with first-year students, highlighting the importance of professional networking, continuous upskilling, certifications, and building a strong LinkedIn presence from the beginning of their academic journey. This collaboration reflects Sanjivani University’s commitment to digital transformation, lifelong learning, industry readiness, and globally benchmarked education—empowering students with the skills and confidence to thrive in a rapidly evolving world.

Sanjivani Students Achieve Global Success with Opportunities in Taiwan, Japan, and Germany

Published On: 06 Aug 2026

Sanjivani Students Achieve Global Success with Opportunities in Taiwan, Japan, and Germany. Sanjivani University and Sanjivani Group of Institutes continue to create global opportunities for students by fostering academic excellence, innovation, and international exposure. Reinforcing its commitment to preparing globally competent professionals, the institution proudly celebrates the outstanding achievement of its students who have secured prestigious international internships and higher education opportunities in Taiwan, Japan, and Germany. These accomplishments are a testament to the students' dedication, hard work, and Sanjivani's vision of empowering learners to excel on the global stage. On behalf of Sanjivani University and Sanjivani Group of Institutes, Kopargaon, we extend our heartfelt congratulations to all the selected students and wish them every success as they embark on this transformative international journey. Selected Students: 1. Ms. Saee Prakash Raskar(Final Year B.Pharm) – Selected for Taiwan. 2. Ms. Ishwari Bhausaheb Anuse(Third Year B.Pharm) – Selected for Taiwan. 3. Ms. Kalyani Jaywant Ghogal(Third Year B.Pharm) – Selected for Taiwan. 4. Mr. Rohit Jaywant Ghogal(B.Pharm) – Selected for Germany. 5. Ms. Gayatri Bhausaheb Shinde(Final Year, Computer Engineering Department, SCOE) – Selected for Taiwan. 6. Ms. Dhanashri Pranil Chaudhari(Final Year, Computer Department, SCOE) – Selected for M.S , Germany. 7. Mr. Pratham Chandrashekhar Gade(Final Year, IT Department, SCOE) – Selected for Japan.

Smart Kopargaon Hackathon 2026

Published On: 17 Jul 2026

Smart Kopargaon Hackathon 2026 Smart Kopargaon Hackathon 2026 is Sanjivani University's flagship national-level innovation challenge, bringing together students from across the country to develop technology-driven solutions for real-world problems aligned with Smart Kopargaon initiatives. Highlights: * Three-stage hybrid hackathon (Online + Offline) * Team of 4 members * Around 50 real-world problem statements * Evaluation by academic and industry experts * Dedicated mentorship throughout the competition * Prize Pool: ₹1,00,000 Registration Opens: 5 July 2026 The Top 100 shortlisted teams will advance to the 30-hour Grand Finale, scheduled for *29–30 August 2026 at *Sanjivani University, Kopargaon, where they will showcase their innovative prototypes before an expert jury. This is an opportunity to solve real-world challenges, strengthen your technical and problem-solving skills, collaborate with talented innovators, and compete for national recognition. Think. Innovate. Build.

Inauguration of the Sanjivani University Tinkerers’ Lab

Published On: 08 Apr 2026

Established with the generous support of the Maker Bhavan Foundation (USA), this state-of-the-art Tinkerers’ Lab is designed to ignite young minds by providing hands-on exposure to design thinking, digital fabrication, Physical AI, IoT, robotics, rapid prototyping, and product development. It will serve as a powerful platform where ideas evolve into solutions and creativity meets real-world impact.

Fortinet Centre of Excellence at Sanjivani University

Published On: 08 Apr 2026

Fortinet Centre of Excellence at Sanjivani University Fortinet, a global leader in cybersecurity solutions, has partnered with Sanjivani University to establish the Fortinet Centre of Excellence on August 28, 2024. This milestone initiative is set to redefine cybersecurity education by providing students with industry-relevant training, real-world project experience, and advanced resources.

Ph.D Admission

Ph.D Admission

Apply Now

Ph.D. Admission at Sanjivani University

Embark on an enriching academic journey with the Ph.D. program at Sanjivani University, designed for aspiring researchers and academicians. Our program offers a unique blend of rigorous research training and interdisciplinary learning in state-of-the-art facilities. With a strong emphasis on innovation, critical thinking, and societal impact, we provide opportunities to collaborate with industry experts and leading academic minds. Join us to contribute to cutting-edge research across diverse disciplines and shape the future of knowledge and innovation. Explore our Ph.D. offerings today and take the first step towards academic excellence!

Why Choose Sanjivani University for Ph.D.?

Comprehensive Support: Dedicated resources, mentorship, and infrastructure to facilitate a seamless research experience.

Innovative Learning Environment: A dynamic campus fostering creativity, critical thinking, and innovation.

Global Network: International collaborations provide opportunities for exposure to diverse perspectives and advanced methodologies.

Societal Impact: Research at Sanjivani University focuses on creating sustainable and impactful solutions for real-world challenges.

Ph.D. Program offered

02 Final Defense (viva voce) Fee (One Time) Rs. 10,000/- Rs. 15,000/-

03 Entrance Exam Application Fee Rs. 1,500/- Rs. 1,500/-

Note: Subject to approval from Fees Fixation Committee.

PRESIDENT'S MESSAGE

Home

President's Message

President's Message

Hon’ble Shri. Amit Nitinrao Kolhe

President’s Desk

President’s Desk

Welcome to Sanjivani University,

Sanjivani University takes pride in providing a top-notch education that prepares learners for success in the real world. Sanjivani's academic fraternity is passionate about their subjects and committed to helping learners reach their full potential. They challenge learners to think critically and develop their curiosity.

The University understands the importance of a relevant education. It designs its programs with inputs from industry experts, so learners graduate with the skills employers seek. The learners gain hands-on experience through internships and projects.

Sanjivani believes in comprehensive student development. The University offers career guidance programs to spark learners’ entrepreneurial spirit and research opportunities. Its goal is to foster individuals who are ready to make a positive impact on society.

Our student exchange programs with top internationally reputed universities broaden their horizons and help them become globally competent. In today's world scenario, understanding different cultures is a major factor in success, and at Sanjivani we provide such an environment with holistic development for all its stakeholders.

At Sanjivani, Sustainability is at the heart of everything we do. The University is committed to creating awareness about the UN's Sustainable Development Goals (SDGs) and empowering the learners to make a positive impact. Whether it's through research or innovative solutions, Sanjivani is committed to encouraging the learners to find a way to create a more sustainable world.

Warm regards,

Hon’ble Shri. Amit Nitinrao Kolhe

President, Sanjivani University

PRIVACY POLICY

Home

Privacy Policy

Privacy Policy

Privacy Policy

Privacy Policy

Effective Date: 12/06/2023

This Privacy Policy governs the manner in which Sanjivani University (“Sanjivani,” “we,” “us,” or “our”) collects, uses, maintains, and discloses information collected from users (“User” or “you”) of the Sanjivani University website (the “Website”). This Privacy Policy applies to the Website and all products and services offered by Sanjivani.

Information Collection

We may collect personal identification information from Users in a variety of ways, including but not limited to when Users visit our Website, fill out a form, subscribe to the newsletter, and in connection with other activities, services, features, or resources we make available on our Website. Users may be asked for their name, email address, mailing address, phone number, and other relevant information. We will collect personal identification information from Users only if they voluntarily submit such information to us. Users can always refuse to supply personal identification information, except that it may prevent them from engaging in certain Website-related activities.

Non-Personal Identification Information

We may collect non-personal identification information about Users whenever they interact with our Website. Non-personal identification information may include the browser name, the type of computer or device, and technical information about Users’ means of connection to our Website, such as the operating system and the Internet service providers utilized, and other similar information.

Web Browser Cookies

Our Website may use “cookies” to enhance User experience. Users’ web browsers place cookies on their hard drive for record-keeping purposes and sometimes to track information about them. Users may choose to set their web browser to refuse cookies or to alert them when cookies are being sent. If they do so, note that some parts of the Website may not function properly.

How We Use Collected Information

Sanjivani may collect and use Users’ personal information for the following purposes:

– To improve customer service: Information you provide helps us respond to your customer service requests and support needs more efficiently.

– To personalize user experience: We may use information in the aggregate to understand how our Users as a group use the services and resources provided on our Website.

– To send periodic emails: We may use the email address to respond to inquiries, questions, and/or other requests.

How We Protect Your Information

We adopt appropriate data collection, storage, and processing practices and security measures to protect against unauthorized access, alteration, disclosure, or destruction of your personal information, username, password, transaction information, and data stored on our Website.

Sharing Your Personal Information

We do not sell, trade, or rent Users’ personal identification information to others. We may share generic aggregated demographic information not linked to any personal identification information regarding visitors and users with our business partners, trusted affiliates, and advertisers for the purposes outlined above.

Changes to This Privacy Policy

Sanjivani has the discretion to update this Privacy Policy at any time. We encourage Users to frequently check this page for any changes to stay informed about how we are helping to protect the personal information we collect. You acknowledge and agree that it is your responsibility to review this Privacy Policy periodically and become aware of modifications.

Your Acceptance of These Terms

By using this Website, you signify your acceptance of this Privacy Policy. If you do not agree to this Privacy Policy, please do not use our Website. Your continued use of the Website following the posting of changes to this Privacy Policy will be deemed your acceptance of those changes.

Contacting Us

If you have any questions about this Privacy Policy, the practices of this Website, or your dealings with this Website, please contact us at:

Sanjivani University

P.O. Shinganapur,

Kopargaon, Tal :Kopargaon,

District: Ahmednagar,

Maharashtra

Pin : 423603

contact@sanjivani.edu.in

Registrar’s Message

Registrar’s Message

Prof. Amol A. Dhakane

Registrar's Desk

Registrar's Desk

Sanjivani University welcomes learners to a world-class infrastructure designed to support their academic success. The University has invested in modern classrooms equipped with multimedia technology, high-tech laboratories, and research facilities to provide learners with the resources they need to excel in their studies and pursue their academic interests.

Sanjivani University is committed to creating a green and safe campus environment. Sustainability is a core value and the university implements eco-friendly practices throughout the campus. Lush green spaces, energy-efficient buildings, and waste management systems contribute to a sustainable and healthy environment for the entire university community.

Furthermore, Sanjivani University equips its labs with state-of-the-art equipment, placing learners at the forefront of technology and allowing them to engage in hands-on learning and research in their chosen fields.

The Registrar's Office, led by Mr. Amol Appasaheb Dhakane, prioritizes student safety and well-being. Strict safety protocols and security measures ensure a secure and nurturing environment where learners can focus on their studies. Encourages learners to explore Sanjivani University with the most of the world-class infrastructure, green campus, and state-of-the-art labs and equipment available to them.

SANJIVANI GROUP LEGACY

Home

Sanjivani Group Legacy

Sanjivani Group Legacy

Sanjivani University

Sanjivani's legacy is defined by excellence, innovation, and global cooperation. Prioritizing academic rigor and research, it fosters a culture of continuous learning. By forging partnerships with renowned universities worldwide, Sanjivani enhances the academic journey for students and facilitates cutting-edge research opportunities for faculty. Yet, its most profound impact lies in its community of scholars, students, and alumni united in driving positive change. Together, they propel Sanjivani as a beacon of knowledge, innovation, and social impact, ensuring a lasting legacy for generations to come.

Sanjivani College Of Engineering & MBA

Sanjivani K.B.P. Polytechnic, Kopargaon

Sanjivani Institute of Management Studies

Sanjivani College of Pharmaceutical and Research

Sanjivani College of Ayurveda and Research Centre (BAMS)

Sanjivani Business School (PGDM)

Sanjivani Senior College, Arts and Commerce

Sanjivani Junior College, Kopargaon

Sanjivani Sainiki School and Jr. College

Sanjivani International School, Shirdi

Sanjivani Academy, Kopargaon

Sanjivani Academy, Vaijapur

Sanjivani Academy, Vaijapur

Sanjivani Academy, Vaijapur is established in year 2020 under the aegis of Sanjivani Rural Education Society. The school is located in Vaijapur, Sambhajinagar district, Maharashtra. It offers education from pre-primary to class X.

Sanjivani Academy is growing under the leadership of Hon’ble Chairman Nitindada Shankarraoji Kolhe (Prominent Educationist and Social Worker), Trustee Hon’ble Shri Sumit Nitinraoji Kolhe and Mrs Nikita Sumit Kolhe as the Managing Director.

The school has a well-qualified and experienced team of teachers who are committed to providing quality education to the students. The school has a modern infrastructure with well-equipped classrooms, laboratories, library, and sports facilities.

Sanjivani Toddlers

Sanjivani Toddlers

Sanjivani Toddlers, the Pre-primary wings of the Sanjivani Group of Institutions, were established in 2015 with the pioneering branch located in Navi Mumbai. Subsequently, toddlers’ branches were opened in Yeola and Shirdi, providing quality education to young children.

The Sanjivani Group of Institutions, established in 1982 by the late Hon. Shankarraoji Kolhe Saheb, is guided by traditional Indian values of devotion, dedication, and determination. With over 34 years of experience in technical education and more than 12 years in schooling, the Sanjivani group has grown into a network of educational institutions across locations such as Navi Mumbai, Kopargaon, Shirdi, and Yeola. The network includes pre-primary schools and primary schools up to the 10th standard, operating under the brand name of Sanjivani Group of Institutes.

Sanjivani University, Kopargaon,

Near Shirdi, Ahilyanagar

(Maharashtra), 423601.

SCHOLARSHIP POLICY

Home

Scholarship Policy

Scholarship Policy

This is the official Scholarship Policy of Sanjivani University and serves as a general framework outlining the university’s provisions, criteria, and procedures for awarding scholarships.

All scholarships offered by the university are subject to the eligibility criteria, availability of funds, and the final approval of the Scholarship Committee and the competent authority of Sanjivani University.

The decisions made by the Scholarship Committee shall be final and binding on all applicants. Sanjivani University reserves the right to revise, modify, suspend, or withdraw any scholarship scheme or its provisions at any time without prior notice, based on regulatory changes or institutional considerations.

Any grievances or disputes related to scholarship applications, selection, or disbursement shall be addressed solely through the University’s Scholarship Committee, in accordance with the prevailing rules and regulations.

Scholarship Policy

SONAI TO TAIWAN VIA SANJIVANI

Home

Sonai To Taiwan Via Sanjivani

Sonai To Taiwan Via Sanjivani

Arya Subhash Jadhav

My research journey to Taiwan marks one of the most defining phases of my academic growth. I had the opportunity to complete a Research Internship at National Chung Hsing University (NCHU), Taichung, Taiwan, under the TEEP Program, where I explored the practical and research-driven aspects of Machine Learning, Deep Learning, and Smart Applications.

At NCHU, I was exposed to hands-on research involving data science, visualization, and the development of ML/DL models, with a focus on real-world applications such as smart agriculture. This experience went far beyond technical learning. It taught me how to think critically, approach problems with a research mindset, collaborate in a global academic environment, and grow both personally and professionally. Taiwan’s vibrant academic ecosystem expanded my perspective and strengthened my aspiration to pursue impactful, research-oriented work.

This journey would not have been possible without the unwavering support of Sanjivani College of Engineering. Sanjivani has always given me the strength and confidence to dream beyond boundaries — encouraging me to look past limitations and believe that global opportunities are achievable with dedication and perseverance. The institution’s culture of academic excellence and global exposure played a vital role in shaping my vision.

The guidance and mentorship I received at Sanjivani helped me find the right direction and clarity to turn this dream into reality. With the right academic path and continuous encouragement, I was able to prepare myself for this international research opportunity with confidence and purpose.

Sanjivani College of Engineering has consistently empowered its students to explore the world of research, innovation, and global learning. This Taiwan internship stands as a reflection of that support system — one that nurtures ambition, provides direction, and inspires students to aim higher.

This experience in Taiwan was not just an academic milestone; it was a step toward realizing a larger vision of contributing meaningfully to the field of AI and ML. I move forward with gratitude, strengthened by the foundation provided by Sanjivani, and motivated to continue pushing boundaries in pursuit of knowledge and impact.

Sanjivani University, Kopargaon,

Near Shirdi, Ahilyanagar

(Maharashtra), 423601.

contact@sanjivani.edu.in

+91 9137700700 / +91 9130191301

SCHOOL OF ENGG. & TECHNOLOGY

School Of Engg. & Technology

Deans Desk

Dr. Kavitha Rani Paramasivan

Dean's Desk (SET)

Dean's Desk (SET)

At Sanjivani University, the School of Engineering and Technology (SET) is committed to nurturing innovation, critical thinking, and technical excellence in the next generation of engineers and technologists. Our programs are designed to equip students with a strong foundation in both theoretical knowledge and practical skills, fostering an environment where curiosity meets cutting-edge technology.

With a dedicated faculty, modern infrastructure, and a curriculum aligned with industry needs, we emphasize experiential learning, interdisciplinary collaboration, and real-world problem-solving. Through our close partnerships with industry leaders, we provide our students with numerous opportunities for internships, live projects, and global exposure, ensuring they are well-prepared for the dynamic challenges of the future.

We believe in shaping not just competent professionals, but innovators and leaders who will contribute meaningfully to society and the technological advancements of tomorrow. I invite you to explore the unique offerings of our school and join us on this exciting journey of learning and growth.

Warm regards,

Dr. Kavitha Rani Paramasivan

Dean, School of Engineering and Technology

Dean of SPS

Welcome to the Sanjivani School of Pharmaceutical Sciences, a distinguished institution under the visionary umbrella of Sanjivani University. We are proud to be a centers of excellence committed to nurturing future pharmacists who are not only academically proficient but also ethically driven and socially responsible.

Pharmaceutical sciences play a critical role in improving global healthcare outcomes. At Sanjivani,we believe in empowering our students through a robust curriculum, hands-on research, industrial collaboration, and a learner-centric environment. Our aim is to cultivate not just competent professionals, but innovators and leaders ready to address the evolving challenges in healthcare and medicine.

Our faculty comprises experienced educators, researchers, and industry professionals who are passionate about student development. We foster an atmosphere of inquiry, creativity, and continuous learning. Through active industry partnerships and state-of-the-art infrastructure, we ensure that our students gain practical exposure that aligns with global standards.

As we move forward in this era of scientific advancement, Sanjivani School of Pharmaceutical Sciences remains steadfast in its mission to contribute to society through academic excellence, research innovation, and community engagement.

I invite you to explore our website and learn more about the programs, achievements, and opportunities that await you at Sanjivani. Join us in our journey toward making a meaningful impact in the world of healthcare.

STUDENT COUNCIL SELECTION PROCESS

Home

Student Council Selection Process

Student Council Selection Process

Student Council Selection Process

Phase 1: Notification

Student Development & Welfare Cell issues a notification regarding Student Council formation.

Eligibility criteria, posts, and timelines are communicated to all students.

Phase 2: Application/Nomination

Interested students submit nomination forms for the desired post.

Faculty recommendations and academic records may be considered.

Phase 3: Eligibility Verification

Applicants must satisfy:

Good academic standing.

Minimum attendance as per university norms.

No disciplinary action pending against them.

Active participation in curricular/co-curricular activities.

Phase 4: Screening & Presentation

Shortlisted candidates present their vision, leadership qualities, and proposed activities before the selection committee.

Interaction/interview may be conducted.

Phase 5: Selection/Election

Depending upon university policy:

Selection Method: Committee constituted by the University selects suitable candidates.

Phase 6: Declaration of Results

Selected candidates are officially announced.

Appointment letters/certificates are issued.

Phase 7: Orientation & Oath Taking

Student Council members undergo orientation regarding their roles and responsibilities.

Oath-taking ceremony is conducted.

STUDENT COUNCIL STRUCTURE

Home

Student Council Structure

Student Council Structure

Introduction

The Student Council is the official representative body of students at Sanjivani University. It serves as a vital link between the student community and the University Administration. The Council aims to promote student welfare, leadership, academic excellence, co-curricular engagement, and active participation in university governance.

The Student Council provides a platform for students to express their views, address concerns, contribute to institutional development, and organize various academic, cultural, sports, social, and community outreach activities. Through collaborative efforts with the University authorities, the Council works towards creating a vibrant, inclusive, and student-centric campus environment.

Objectives of the Student Council

To represent the interests and concerns of students before the University authorities.

To promote student participation in academic, cultural, sports, research, innovation, and social activities.

To strengthen communication between students and the University administration.

To develop leadership, teamwork, and organizational skills among students.

To support and coordinate student clubs, forums, and societies.

To encourage community engagement and social responsibility.

To foster a positive and inclusive campus culture.

Functions of the Student Council

Representation

Act as the voice of students in various University committees and decision-making bodies.

Communicate student concerns, suggestions, and feedback to the administration.

Student Welfare

Address issues related to academics, facilities, student services, and campus life.

Promote initiatives for student well-being and development.

Event Management

Organize academic, technical, cultural, sports, social, and community outreach programs.

Encourage active participation of students in University activities.

Leadership Development

Provide opportunities for students to develop leadership and management skills.

Encourage responsible citizenship and ethical conduct.

Collaboration

Work closely with University authorities, faculty coordinators, clubs, forums, and student organizations.

Student Council Structure

The Student Council shall consist of:

President

Vice President

General Secretary

Treasurer

Cultural Secretary

Sports Secretary

University Magazine Secretary

NSS Secretary

NCC Secretary

Ladies Representative

School/Department Representatives

Hostel Representative (if applicable)

Club and Forum Representatives

Powers and Duties of Student Council Members

President

The President shall be the head of the Student Council and shall:

Lead the Student Council throughout the tenure.

Preside over all Student Council meetings.

Represent students before the University authorities.

Coordinate with the Vice-Chancellor, Registrar, Deans, Directors, and other officials on matters concerning students.

Supervise the functioning of all student clubs, forums, and activities.

Approve major student activities and initiatives.

Guide and monitor all Student Council members in the discharge of their duties.

Serve as the student representative on University committees as nominated.

Ensure discipline, transparency, and accountability within the Council.

Assist in the smooth conduct of Student Council elections and transition to the next Council.

Vice President

The Vice President shall:

Assist the President in all Council activities.

Perform the duties of the President in the latter's absence.

Coordinate meetings and prepare meeting agendas.

Support planning and execution of student programs.

Maintain official records and documentation of Council activities.

Coordinate with all Council members and faculty coordinators.

Assist in budget planning and resource allocation.

Monitor implementation of Council decisions.

General Secretary

The General Secretary shall:

Maintain minutes and records of all Student Council meetings.

Draft official correspondence and communications.

Coordinate communication between students and University authorities.

Maintain records of student clubs, forums, and activities.

Assist the President and Vice President in execution of Council functions.

Ensure timely circulation of notices, agendas, and reports.

Act as the approving authority in the absence of the President.

Treasurer

The Treasurer shall:

Prepare and manage the Student Council budget.

Maintain records of all financial transactions.

Monitor utilization of allocated funds.

Coordinate with the Accounts Department regarding financial matters.

Prepare expenditure statements and utilization reports.

Assist clubs and forums in budget planning.

Ensure transparency and accountability in financial management.

Prepare the Annual Student Activity Calendar in consultation with the President and Vice President.

Cultural Secretary

The Cultural Secretary shall:

Coordinate all cultural clubs and forums of the University.

Plan and organize cultural festivals, competitions, and events.

Promote student participation in inter-university cultural activities.

Coordinate publicity and promotional activities for cultural programs.

Assist in sponsorship and resource mobilization for events.

Prepare annual cultural activity plans and budgets.

Sports Secretary

The Sports Secretary shall:

Coordinate all sports and fitness-related activities.

Organize intra-university and inter-university sports events.

Encourage student participation in sports competitions.

Coordinate with the Sports Department and coaches.

Prepare sports activity plans and budgets.

Promote health, fitness, and sportsmanship among students.

University Magazine Secretary

The University Magazine Secretary shall:

Coordinate preparation and publication of the University Magazine.

Collect articles, reports, and creative content from various Schools and Departments.

Work with editorial teams and faculty coordinators.

Ensure quality, originality, and timely publication of the magazine.

Serve as Student Editor-in-Chief for University publications.

NSS Secretary

The NSS Secretary shall:

Coordinate all National Service Scheme (NSS) activities.

Promote social responsibility and community engagement.

Organize awareness campaigns, community service projects, and outreach activities.

Encourage student participation in NSS programs.

Maintain records and reports of NSS activities.

Collaborate with NSS Programme Officers and volunteers.

NCC Secretary

The NCC Secretary shall:

Coordinate all National Cadet Corps (NCC) activities.

Encourage student participation in NCC programs and camps.

Assist in maintaining discipline and leadership development initiatives.

Coordinate with NCC officers and cadets.

Support national service and patriotic activities on campus.

Tenure of the Student Council

The tenure of the Student Council shall normally be one academic year or as prescribed by the University from time to time.

Code of Conduct

All Student Council members shall:

Uphold the dignity and reputation of the University.

Maintain discipline and ethical conduct.

Promote inclusiveness, equality, and mutual respect.

Work in the best interests of the student community.

Ensure transparency and accountability in all activities.

Comply with University rules, regulations, and policies.

Conclusion

The Student Council of Sanjivani University serves as an important platform for student leadership, participation, and governance. Through active engagement, responsible leadership, and collaboration with the University administration, the Council contributes significantly to the holistic development of students and the enhancement of campus life.

Sanjivani University, Kopargaon,

Near Shirdi, Ahilyanagar

(Maharashtra), 423601.

contact@sanjivani.edu.in

+91 9137700700 / +91 9130191301
