Ph.D.

Sanjivani Institute of Pharmacy
and Research (D. Pharmacy)

Established in September 2003, the Diploma in Pharmacy program addresses the
escalating demand for skilled professionals in the pharmaceutical ﬁeld. The department
focuses on fundamental academic and Industrial requirements, encompassing Drug
Procurement, Isolation, Manufacturing, Identiﬁcation, Discovery, Modiﬁcation, Dispensing,
Storage, Analysis, and understanding the effects of drugs on the body. With a committed
and qualiﬁed staff, the department is renowned for maintaining high academic standards
and discipline, ensuring students acquire comprehensive knowledge in pharmacy to meet
the industry's evolving needs amid the fast-paced global landscape.

Established in 2003, plays a vital role in addressing the foundational academic and
industrial needs of the pharmaceutical ﬁeld. Its primary focus is to impart comprehensive
knowledge to students, encompassing various facets such as Drug Procurement, Isolation,
Manufacturing, Identiﬁcation, Discovery, Modiﬁcation, Dispensing, Storage, Analysis, and
understanding the effects and precautions related to drug use. This holistic approach
addresses the interplay between drugs and the human body. Renowned for its academic
excellence, the department boasts a dedicated and highly qualiﬁed staff, maintaining
disciplined standards and contributing signiﬁcantly to the advancement of pharmacy
education.

PLACEMENT PROCESS
The Corporate Relations & Placement team sends placement invitation to the
companies enclosing a brief summary of the courses available at Sanjivani.
On receiving the job requirement, the CRP team announces the requirement of the
company, asking interested students to submit their resumes of marks citeria, which
The company, after short listing candidates from the application pool and eligibility
data ﬁle sent to them, sends back the list of short listed candidates to the CRPT at the
earliest.
A mutually convenient date is ﬁnalized for the campus recruitment drive.
After completion of the selection process, the company is required to furnish the ﬁnal
list of selected students preferably on the same day, or as soon as possible.
The company sends offer letters to the CRPT for handing them over to the students.
Offer acceptances (from the students) are received and sent to the company by the
CRPT.
Our Campus Hiring Program starts by the beginning of the
Pre- Final semester. The Placement Process at Sanjivani
includes some or all of below-mentioned activities:

SANJIVANI
UNIVERSITY

# Security

![img-0.jpeg](img-0.jpeg)

![img-1.jpeg](img-1.jpeg)

![img-2.jpeg](img-2.jpeg)

1. 24×7 Campus Surveillance

Continuous monitoring through CCTV cameras installed at strategic locations across the campus.

2. Trained Security Personnel

Well-trained and experienced security staff deployed round-the-clock at all entry and exit points.

3. Controlled Entry & Exit

Strict access control for students, staff, visitors, and vehicles with proper verification.

4. ID Card Verification

Mandatory identity cards for students, faculty, staff, and authorized visitors.

5. Women Safety Measures

Dedicated security arrangements ensuring a safe and secure environment for female students and staff.

6. Emergency Response System

Quick-response protocols in place for medical emergencies, fire safety, and unforeseen situations.

7. Fire Safety & Disaster Management

Fire extinguishers, alarms, evacuation plans, and periodic mock drills conducted as per safety norms.

8. Hostel Security

Separate and secure arrangements for boys' and girls' hostels with wardens and night security.

9. Vehicle & Parking Security

Regulated vehicle movement with designated parking zones and monitoring.

10. Visitor Management System

Entry allowed only after proper registration and authorization.

11. Coordination with Local Authorities

Regular coordination with local police, fire department, and medical services.

12. Anti-Ragging & Discipline Monitoring

Strict implementation of anti-ragging policies supported by surveillance and security checks.

13. Digital Security Support

Monitoring of IT infrastructure to ensure cyber safety and data protection.

14. Regular Security Audits

Periodic reviews and upgrades of security systems to meet evolving safety requirements.

15. Safe & Peaceful Learning Environment

Commitment to providing a secure, disciplined, and student-friendly campus atmosphere.


SOURCE URL: https://sanjivani.edu.in/uploads/Security.pdf

Security
1. 24×7 Campus Surveillance
Continuous monitoring through CCTV cameras installed at
strategic locations across the campus.
2.Trained Security Personnel
Well-trained and experienced security staff deployed
round-the-clock at all entry and exit points.
3.Controlled Entry & Exit
Strict access control for students, staff, visitors, and
vehicles with proper verification.
4. ID Card Verification
Mandatory identity cards for students, faculty, staff, and
authorized visitors.
5.Women Safety Measures
Dedicated security arrangements ensuring a safe and
secure environment for female students and staff.
6.Emergency Response System
Quick-response protocols in place for medical emergencies,
fire safety, and unforeseen situations.
7.Fire Safety & Disaster Management
Fire extinguishers, alarms, evacuation plans, and periodic
mock drills conducted as per safety norms.
8.Hostel Security
Separate and secure arrangements for boys’ and girls’
hostels with wardens and night security.
9.Vehicle & Parking Security
Regulated vehicle movement with designated parking zones
and monitoring.
10.Visitor Management System
Entry allowed only after proper registration and
authorization.
11.Coordination with Local Authorities
Regular coordination with local police, fire department, and
medical services.
12.Anti-Ragging & Discipline Monitoring
Strict implementation of anti-ragging policies supported by
surveillance and security checks.
13. Digital Security Support
Monitoring of IT infrastructure to ensure cyber safety and
data protection.
14.Regular Security Audits
Periodic reviews and upgrades of security systems to meet
evolving safety requirements.
15.Safe & Peaceful Learning Environment
Commitment to providing a secure, disciplined, and student-
friendly campus atmosphere.

# web_sanjivani_edu_in_uploads_Student_Council_2026_27_Notice_pdf

## Page 1

SANJIVANI
UNIVERSITY

# Sanjivani University

Student Development & Welfare Cell

Student Activity Council 2026-27

Ref No: SU/SDW/2026-27/01

Date: 13/08/2026

# Notice

All students of Sanjivani University are hereby informed that the Student Development & Welfare Department is initiating the process of forming the Student Council for the academic year 2026-27. Interested students are requested to submit a written application along with the signature of respective Department SDWC coordinator, HoD and Dean of the school on or before 18th-Aug- 2026, by 05:00 PM. Applications must be submitted to the respective school coordinators and also scan the application after signature and upload on the google form https://forms.gle/ZYjXi246EY2ianZn9. The selection for various positions is based on the test (online/offline), interview process and relevant experience.

# Important Instructions for Student Council Applicants:

1. The Student Council Application Form (PDF) and Google Form link are now available on the University website. Students can access them by navigating to: Student Corner \(\rightarrow\) Student Welfare Section.
2. Application Submission: Submission of the hard copy of the duly filled and signed application form to the respective Department SDW Coordinator is mandatory. In addition, students must upload a scanned copy of the signed application form through the prescribed Google Form before the specified deadline. Both submissions are compulsory.
3. Students should submit only one application for the Student Council.
4. Previous Council Members - Students who have served as Student Council members for the previous two years in the same position/post are not eligible to apply for the same position in the current academic year. Applications submitted for the same position will not be considered.
5. Submission of the application does not guarantee selection. Applicants may be required to attend an interview, presentation, interaction, or other selection process.
6. Applicants must regularly check their registered email, WhatsApp groups/notices, and university announcements for further instructions.
7. Academic Responsibility: Student Council responsibilities must be managed without adversely affecting academic performance and attendance.
8. Final Decision: The decision of the Student Development & Welfare Cell/University authorities regarding eligibility, selection, and allocation of positions shall be final.
9. Enquiry: For any clarification regarding the application or selection process, students should contact the Student Development & Welfare Cell (SDWC).

Post Available in the Student Council-

9130191301

सबका माʁलक एक
|| ॐ ±ी साɠनाथाय नमः ||

Late Shankarrao Genuji Kolhe Saheb (1929-2022) was a
dynamic force for Maharashtra's rural development, leaving an
indelible legacy in agriculture, public service, and community
upliftment. Beginning his journey as an agriculturist by training,
Saheb initially entered public service as a village headman
(Sarpanch), eventually serving multiple terms as a member of the
Maharashtra Legislative Assembly and as a Minister in the State
Government. Throughout his career, he tirelessly advocated for
farmers’ rights and championed policies aimed at improving the
quality of life in rural communities.

Beyond politics, Saheb was a visionary in education and
economic development. He was instrumental in founding numerous
Educational Institutions and cooperative industries, driving both
social and economic progress across rural Maharashtra. Under his
leadership, twelve educational campuses ﬂourished, reﬂecting his
deep commitment to education as a tool for empowerment. Saheb’s
dedication to social progress and rural advancement continues to
inspire countless individuals, ensuring his legacy lives on.

In the pursuit of excellence in education and global
collaboration, Sanjivani University, under the leadership of its
dedicated chairman, is committed to bringing quality international
education to its students' doorsteps. Through strategic partnerships
with leading Institutions worldwide, the University oﬀers diverse
programs and opportunities for students to engage with
International faculty, exchange ideas with peers from diﬀerent
cultures, and gain valuable insights into global issues. Beyond the
classroom, Sanjivani University provides state-of-the- art
facilities and hands-on learning experiences to ensure holistic
development and prepare students for success in today's
competitive landscape. With a focus on equipping students with
the knowledge, skills, and mind set needed to excel in their chosen
ﬁelds, Sanjivani University is dedicated to building a brighter
future for its community and the world at large.
Message From The Chairman’s Desk
Late Shankarraoji Kolhe Saheb
Former Minister,
Government of Maharashtra
Hon. Shri. Nitinrao Shankarrao Kolhe
Chairman
Sanjivani Rural Education Society
The Pioneer of Socio-Economic Transformation
FOUNDER

Sanjivani University takes pride in providing a top-notch
education that prepares learners for success in the real world.
Sanjivani's academic fraternity is passionate about their subjects and
committed to helping learners reach their full potential. They
challenge learners to think critically and develop their curiosity. The
University understands the importance of a relevant education. It
designs its programs with inputs from industry experts, so learners
graduate with the skills employers seek. The Learners gain hands-on
experience through foreign internships and projects.

About Sanjivani University

Founded in 1983, the Sanjivani Group of Institutes is a renowned educational organization oﬀering
programs in engineering, management, pharmacy, polytechnic, and more. Known for academic excellence and
holistic development, it stands among Maharashtra's top institutions.

An extension of this legacy, Sanjivani University combines innovation with interdisciplinary education.
Through industry-driven programs, skill development, and a modern curriculum, it prepares students for global
challenges, driven by a mission to leverage technology and science for socio-economic growth.

## Page 3

# 1. INTRODUCTION

The Scholarship Policy of Sanjivani University is designed to recognize academic excellence, promote inclusivity, and support students from diverse socio-economic backgrounds. This policy establishes a structured and transparent framework for awarding scholarships to meritorious, deserving, and economically weaker students across all academic programs. It defines the types of scholarships available, eligibility criteria, application procedures, and disbursement guidelines. The policy aligns with the university’s vision of fostering a culture of academic achievement, equity, and social responsibility while adhering to the norms of statutory bodies and government directives.

# 2. ABOUT SANJIVANI UNIVERSITY

Sanjivani University, established by the Sanjivani Rural Education Society under the Maharashtra State Private Universities Act no XX of 2024, located in Kopargaon, near the holy town of Shirdi. With a legacy of over four decades in delivering quality education, Sanjivani has grown into a trusted name across Maharashtra and beyond. The university offers a wide range of undergraduate, postgraduate and doctoral programs, through its diverse School of Engineering and Technology, School of Commerce and Management, School of Pharmaceutical Science and School of Sciences. With a clear focus on academic excellence, industry integration, and global exposure, the university is equipped with smart classrooms, advanced laboratories, high-speed connectivity, incubation centers, and vibrant hostel and sports facilities. Backed by a highly qualified faculty, modern pedagogy, and value-based education, Sanjivani University is committed to nurturing future-ready professionals with strong ethical foundations. The institution actively promotes research, innovation, entrepreneurship, and skill development, while also encouraging social responsibility through community outreach and rural immersion initiatives.

# 3. VISION and MISSION

## VISION:

- To emerge as a globally recognized university for excellence in Education, Research, Innovation and Entrepreneurship in order to produce effective leaders for serving the society.

## MISSION:

- Providing state of the art infrastructure, industry centric curriculum and education focusing on ethics and critical thinking

---

## Page 4

- Promoting an innovative and research-oriented environment for fostering effective problem solving and entrepreneurial development
- Collaborating with National and International Industries and Higher Education Institutions for academics and research
- Creating a conducive environment for a vibrant, happy and responsible society by contributing to Institutional Social Responsibility (ISR) initiatives

#### 4. PURPOSE

The Meritorious Scholarship offered by Sanjivani University aims to recognize and reward academic excellence among students. This scholarship is instituted to encourage a culture of high achievement, inspire consistent academic performance, and support deserving students in pursuing higher education without financial constraints. By fostering a spirit of competitiveness and dedication, the scholarship serves as a catalyst for nurturing future leaders, innovators, and professionals committed to excellence and societal development.

#### 5. SCHOLARSHIPS AND FEES CONCESSION

<table><thead><tr><th>SN</th><th>Name of Scholarship</th><th>% of Fees award</th><th>% of Intake</th></tr></thead><tr><td>01</td><td>Shri. Genuji Kolhe Saheb Merit-based Tuition Waiver Scheme</td><td>100% tuition fess waiver</td><td>2.5% of Admitted Students</td></tr><tr><td>02</td><td>Sahakarmaharshi Shankarraoji Kolhe Saheb Vidyadhan Scholarship for Economically Weaker Meritorious Students</td><td>50% tuition fees Waiver</td><td>10% of Admitted Students</td></tr><tr><td>03</td><td>Smt. Laxmibai Kolhe (Maai), Vidya Gaurav Scholarship</td><td>20% Tuition Fess Waiver</td><td>5% of Admitted Students</td></tr></table>

#### Rules and Regulations Governing the University Scholarships Schemes: -

The following rules and regulations shall govern the award, continuation, and administration of scholarships conferred by the Sanjivani University for meritorious students:

##### 1. Merit-Based Selection:

The award of all scholarships shall be purely merit-based, subject to academic performance and compliance with other prescribed conditions.

##### 2. Display of Provisional List:

A provisional list of eligible candidates shall be published for informational purposes only. Inclusion in this list does not constitute a guarantee or confirmation of scholarship award.

---

## Page 5

# **3. Final Award List:**

The final list of recipients shall be published only upon verification of requisite documents and satisfaction of all prescribed eligibility criteria.

# **4. Minimum CGPA Requirement for Continuation:**

Scholarship holders shall be required to maintain the following minimum Cumulative Grade Point Average (CGPA) for continued eligibility in subsequent academic years. Failure to do so shall result in automatic and irreversible termination of the scholarship from that academic year onward.

<table><thead><tr><th>Sr.<br/>No.</th><th>Name of Scholarship</th><th>Minimum CGPA to be<br/>Maintain</th></tr></thead><tr><td>1.</td><td>Shri. Genuji Kolhe Saheb Merit-based Tuition Waiver Scheme</td><td><b>8.5 and above</b></td></tr><tr><td>2.</td><td>Sahakarmaharshi Shankarraoji Kolhe Saheb Vidyadhan Scholarship for Economically Weaker Meritorious Students</td><td><b>8.0 and above</b></td></tr><tr><td>3.</td><td>Smt. Laxmibai Kolhe (Maai), Vidya Gaurav Scholarship</td><td><b>7.5 and above</b></td></tr></table>

# **5. Code of Conduct:**

Scholarship recipients shall strictly adhere to the Code of Conduct and academic integrity policies of the University. Any act of indiscipline, misconduct, or violation shall render the recipient liable for immediate cancellation of the scholarship, without further consideration.

# **6. Immutability of Scheme:**

The scholarship scheme awarded at the time of admission shall remain unchanged throughout the duration of the programme. No modifications or transitions between schemes shall be permitted under any circumstances.

# **7. Restriction on Additional Awardees:**

No new students shall be inducted or added into the scholarship scheme from the second year onwards.

# **8. Minimum Attendance Requirement:**

A minimum of 75% attendance in each academic term is mandatory for the continuation of the scholarship. Failure to comply shall result in cancellation.

# **9. Family Annual Income Requirement:**

- **Shri Genuji Kolhe Saheb Merit-based Tuition Waiver Scheme**

→ *There is no family annual income requirement.*

---

## Page 6

- Smt. Laxmibai Kolhe (Maai) Vidya Gaurav Scholarship
→ There is no family annual income requirement.
- Sahakarmaharshi Shankarraoji Kolhe Saheb Vidyadhan Scholarship for Economically Weaker Meritorious Students
→ To be eligible, the annual family income must be less than ₹8,00,000.

10. Renewal of Scholarship:

Subject to continued eligibility, compliance with the above rules, and satisfactory academic performance, the scholarship may be renewed annually.

11. Grievance Redressal:

Any queries or grievances regarding the scholarship must be submitted in writing to registrar@sanjivani.edu.in within seven (07) days from the date of publication of the scholarship list. No complaints received after this period shall be entertained.

12. Approval and Sanctioning Authority:

All scholarships shall be subject to scrutiny and final approval by the University Admission Committee and the Competent Authority of Sanjivani University.

13. Finality of Decisions:

The decisions of the Admission Committee concerning the award, continuation, or cancellation of scholarships shall be final, conclusive, and binding on all applicants and recipients.

14. Right to Amend:

Sanjivani University reserves the right to modify, amend, suspend, or withdraw any provision herein, without prior notice, as deemed necessary in the context of changing regulations, policies, or institutional requirements.

Fee Concession Policy to Wards of Sanjivani University / SRES Staff Members

In recognition of the committed service rendered by its employees, Sanjivani University, under the aegis of Sanjivani Rural Education Society (SRES), extends a fee concession policy to the wards and spouses of eligible full-time staff and faculty members. The terms and conditions governing the grant and continuation of such concession are as follows:

I. Eligibility Criteria

1. The tuition fee concession shall be applicable exclusively to:

- Wards (sons/daughters) or
- Spouses

of full-time staff or faculty members serving under Sanjivani Rural Education Society or any of its constituent colleges/institutes, including Sanjivani University.

---

## Page 7

2. The employee must have completed a minimum of three (03) continuous years of full-time service as of the date of admission of the beneficiary.

## **II. Concession Details**

1. A concession amounting to 50% of the tuition fee shall be granted for the entire duration of the admitted academic program, subject to fulfilment of all prescribed conditions.
2. The fee concession shall not cover other components such as examination fees, development fees, hostel charges, transport, or any other institutional levies, unless otherwise specified.

## **III. Minimum Academic Requirement**

To be eligible for the fee concession, the student must have secured a minimum of 60% aggregate marks in the qualifying examination required for admission to the respective program.

## **IV. Withdrawal and Forfeiture Clause**

1. The fee concession shall stand revoked with immediate effect in the event of any of the following:
- Resignation of the concerned staff member
- Termination or compulsory retirement of the staff member
- Discontinuation of service for any reason whatsoever during the academic year.
2. Upon such revocation, the student shall become liable to pay the full tuition fee for the respective academic year in which the discontinuity occurred.

## **V. Additional Conditions**

1. In order to avail the fee concession under this policy, the concerned faculty or staff member shall be required to execute a formal undertaking/bond affirming that he/she will continue to serve the organization for a minimum period of three (03) years after the completion of the academic program by the ward/spouse.
2. In the event of voluntary resignation, termination, or cessation of service by the employee before the completion of the mandatory service period post-course completion, the entire fee concession availed under this policy shall become immediately recoverable from the employee. The amount shall be treated as a financial liability and may be deducted from salary dues, terminal benefits, or by legal recovery if necessary.
3. The University reserves the right to initiate legal or administrative action for recovery of such dues in the event of non-compliance with the conditions of the bond/undertaking.

---

## Page 8

4. This concession is non-transferable and shall apply only to one beneficiary per employee at a time.
5. In case of re-admission, course extension, or year drop, the scholarship will be discontinued.
6. The concession is subject to the final approval of the University Admission Committee and competent authorities of Sanjivani University.
7. Sanjivani University reserves the right to amend, withdraw, or modify any clause of this policy at any time without prior notice, in accordance with institutional priorities and regulatory changes.

## **6. SCHOLARSHIP COMMITTEE**

There shall be a Scholarship Committee constituted by Sanjivani University to regulate, evaluate, and administer all scholarship schemes offered by the university. The committee shall function independently or in coordination with the Admission Committee, wherever required, to ensure effective implementation of the scholarship policy.

The Scholarship Committee shall comprise the following members:

1. Registrar- Chairman
2. Deans of respective School- Member
3. Controller of Examination- Member
4. Director ERP- Member
5. Chief Account & Finance officer- Member
6. Director Admission- Member Secretary

### **Functions and Responsibilities**

- Formulate, review, and update scholarship guidelines and procedures in alignment with university objectives and statutory requirements.
- Screen and evaluate scholarship applications based on predefined eligibility criteria, academic merit, socio-economic background, and other relevant parameters.
- Recommend deserving students for various types of scholarships such as merit-based, need-based, special-category, and externally funded schemes.
- Verify submitted documents for authenticity and completeness before final approval.
- Coordinate with the Finance Department for timely disbursement of scholarship funds to selected students.
- Maintain comprehensive records of all scholarship applications, approvals, rejections, and fund utilization.
- Address grievances or appeals related to scholarship allocation, if any, in a timely and impartial manner.

---

## Page 9

- Prepare periodic reports and analytics on scholarship distribution for review by the Academic Council or university management.
- Co-opt subject experts or departmental representatives, when necessary, to assist in the evaluation process.
- The decisions made by the Scholarship Committee shall be final and binding in all scholarship-related matters.

## **7. SCHOLARSHIP POLICY DISCLAIMER**

- This is the official Scholarship Policy of Sanjivani University and serves as a general framework outlining the university’s provisions, criteria, and procedures for awarding scholarships.
- All scholarships offered by the university are subject to the eligibility criteria, availability of funds, and the final approval of the Scholarship Committee and the competent authority of Sanjivani University.
- The decisions made by the Scholarship Committee shall be final and binding on all applicants. Sanjivani University reserves the right to revise, modify, suspend, or withdraw any scholarship scheme or its provisions at any time without prior notice, based on regulatory changes or institutional considerations.
- Any grievances or disputes related to scholarship applications, selection, or disbursement shall be addressed solely through the University’s Scholarship Committee, in accordance with the prevailing rules and regulations.

SCHOLARSHIP POLICY
w.e.f. 2024-25

INDEX
Particulars
Page Numbers
1. Introduction
2. About Sanjivani University
3. Vision and Mission
4. Purpose
5. Scholarships and Fees Concession

7. Scholarship Policy Disclaimer

1. INTRODUCTION
The Scholarship Policy of Sanjivani University is designed to recognize academic excellence,
promote inclusivity, and support students from diverse socio-economic backgrounds. This
policy establishes a structured and transparent framework for awarding scholarships to
meritorious, deserving, and economically weaker students across all academic programs. It
defines the types of scholarships available, eligibility criteria, application procedures, and
disbursement guidelines. The policy aligns with the university’s vision of fostering a culture
of academic achievement, equity, and social responsibility while adhering to the norms of
statutory bodies and government directives.

2. ABOUT SANJIVANI UNIVERSITY
Sanjivani University, established by the Sanjivani Rural Education Society under the
Maharashtra State Private Universities Act no XX of 2024, located in Kopargaon, near the
holy town of Shirdi. With a legacy of over four decades in delivering quality education,
Sanjivani has grown into a trusted name across Maharashtra and beyond. The university offers
a wide range of undergraduate, postgraduate and doctoral programs, through its diverse School
of Engineering and Technology, School of Commerce and Management, School of
Pharmaceutical Science and School of Sciences. With a clear focus on academic excellence,
industry integration, and global exposure, the university is equipped with smart classrooms,
advanced laboratories, high-speed connectivity, incubation centers, and vibrant hostel and
sports facilities. Backed by a highly qualified faculty, modern pedagogy, and value-based
education, Sanjivani University is committed to nurturing future-ready professionals with
strong ethical foundations. The institution actively promotes research, innovation,
entrepreneurship, and skill development, while also encouraging social responsibility through
community outreach and rural immersion initiatives.

3. VISION and MISSION
VISION:
• To emerge as a globally recognized university for excellence in Education, Research,
Innovation and Entrepreneurship in order to produce effective leaders for serving the
society.
MISSION:
• Providing state of the art infrastructure, industry centric curriculum and education
focusing on ethics and critical thinking

• Promoting an innovative and research-oriented environment for fostering effective
problem solving and entrepreneurial development
• Collaborating with National and International Industries and Higher Education
Institutions for academics and research
• Creating a conducive environment for a vibrant, happy and responsible society by
contributing to Institutional Social Responsibility (ISR) initiatives

4. PURPOSE
The Meritorious Scholarship offered by Sanjivani University aims to recognize and reward
academic excellence among students. This scholarship is instituted to encourage a culture of
high achievement, inspire consistent academic performance, and support deserving students
in pursuing higher education without financial constraints. By fostering a spirit of
competitiveness and dedication, the scholarship serves as a catalyst for nurturing future
leaders, innovators, and professionals committed to excellence and societal development.

Rules and Regulations Governing the University Scholarships Schemes: -
The following rules and regulations shall govern the award, continuation, and administration
of scholarships conferred by the Sanjivani University for meritorious students:
1. Merit-Based Selection:
The award of all scholarships shall be purely merit-based, subject to academic performance
and compliance with other prescribed conditions.
2. Display of Provisional List:
A provisional list of eligible candidates shall be published for informational purposes only.
Inclusion in this list does not constitute a guarantee or confirmation of scholarship award.

3. Final Award List:
The final list of recipients shall be published only upon verification of requisite documents
and satisfaction of all prescribed eligibility criteria.
4. Minimum CGPA Requirement for Continuation:
Scholarship holders shall be required to maintain the following minimum Cumulative
Grade Point Average (CGPA) for continued eligibility in subsequent academic years.
Failure to do so shall result in automatic and irreversible termination of the scholarship
from that academic year onward.
Sr.

Name of Scholarship
Minimum CGPA to be
Maintain

7.5 and above

5. Code of Conduct:
Scholarship recipients shall strictly adhere to the Code of Conduct and academic integrity
policies of the University. Any act of indiscipline, misconduct, or violation shall render
the recipient liable for immediate cancellation of the scholarship, without further
consideration.
6. Immutability of Scheme:
The scholarship scheme awarded at the time of admission shall remain unchanged
throughout the duration of the programme. No modifications or transitions between
schemes shall be permitted under any circumstances.
7. Restriction on Additional Awardees:
No new students shall be inducted or added into the scholarship scheme from the second
year onwards.
8. Minimum Attendance Requirement:
A minimum of 75% attendance in each academic term is mandatory for the continuation
of the scholarship. Failure to comply shall result in cancellation.
9. Family Annual Income Requirement:
• Shri Genuji Kolhe Saheb Merit-based Tuition Waiver Scheme
→ There is no family annual income requirement.

• Smt. Laxmibai Kolhe (Maai) Vidya Gaurav Scholarship
→ There is no family annual income requirement.
• Sahakarmaharshi Shankarraoji Kolhe Saheb Vidyadhan Scholarship for
Economically Weaker Meritorious Students
→ To be eligible, the annual family income must be less than ₹8,00,000.
10. Renewal of Scholarship:
Subject to continued eligibility, compliance with the above rules, and satisfactory
academic performance, the scholarship may be renewed annually.
11. Grievance Redressal:
Any queries or grievances regarding the scholarship must be submitted in writing to
registrar@sanjivani.edu.in within seven (07) days from the date of publication of the
scholarship list. No complaints received after this period shall be entertained.
12. Approval and Sanctioning Authority:
All scholarships shall be subject to scrutiny and final approval by the University Admission
Committee and the Competent Authority of Sanjivani University.
13. Finality of Decisions:
The decisions of the Admission Committee concerning the award, continuation, or
cancellation of scholarships shall be final, conclusive, and binding on all applicants and
recipients.
14. Right to Amend:
Sanjivani University reserves the right to modify, amend, suspend, or withdraw any
provision herein, without prior notice, as deemed necessary in the context of changing
regulations, policies, or institutional requirements.

Fee Concession Policy to Wards of Sanjivani University / SRES Staff Members
In recognition of the committed service rendered by its employees, Sanjivani University, under
the aegis of Sanjivani Rural Education Society (SRES), extends a fee concession policy to the
wards and spouses of eligible full-time staff and faculty members. The terms and conditions
governing the grant and continuation of such concession are as follows:
I. Eligibility Criteria
1. The tuition fee concession shall be applicable exclusively to:
o Wards (sons/daughters) or
o Spouses
of full-time staff or faculty members serving under Sanjivani Rural Education
Society or any of its constituent colleges/institutes, including Sanjivani University.

2. The employee must have completed a minimum of three (03) continuous years of full-time
service as of the date of admission of the beneficiary.

II. Concession Details
1. A concession amounting to 50% of the tuition fee shall be granted for the entire duration
of the admitted academic program, subject to fulfilment of all prescribed conditions.
2. The fee concession shall not cover other components such as examination fees,
development fees, hostel charges, transport, or any other institutional levies, unless
otherwise specified.
III. Minimum Academic Requirement
To be eligible for the fee concession, the student must have secured a minimum of 60%
aggregate marks in the qualifying examination required for admission to the respective
program.

IV. Withdrawal and Forfeiture Clause
1. The fee concession shall stand revoked with immediate effect in the event of any of the
following:
o Resignation of the concerned staff member
o Termination or compulsory retirement of the staff member
o Discontinuation of service for any reason whatsoever during the academic year.
2. Upon such revocation, the student shall become liable to pay the full tuition fee for the
respective academic year in which the discontinuity occurred.

V. Additional Conditions
1. In order to avail the fee concession under this policy, the concerned faculty or staff member
shall be required to execute a formal undertaking/bond affirming that he/she will continue
to serve the organization for a minimum period of three (03) years after the completion of
the academic program by the ward/spouse.
2. In the event of voluntary resignation, termination, or cessation of service by the employee
before the completion of the mandatory service period post-course completion, the entire
fee concession availed under this policy shall become immediately recoverable from the
employee. The amount shall be treated as a financial liability and may be deducted from
salary dues, terminal benefits, or by legal recovery if necessary.
3. The University reserves the right to initiate legal or administrative action for recovery of
such dues in the event of non-compliance with the conditions of the bond/undertaking.

4. This concession is non-transferable and shall apply only to one beneficiary per employee
at a time.
5. In case of re-admission, course extension, or year drop, the scholarship will be
discontinued.
6. The concession is subject to the final approval of the University Admission Committee
and competent authorities of Sanjivani University.
7. Sanjivani University reserves the right to amend, withdraw, or modify any clause of this
policy at any time without prior notice, in accordance with institutional priorities and
regulatory changes.
6. SCHOLARSHIP COMMITTEE
There shall be a Scholarship Committee constituted by Sanjivani University to regulate,
evaluate, and administer all scholarship schemes offered by the university. The committee
shall function independently or in coordination with the Admission Committee, wherever
required, to ensure effective implementation of the scholarship policy.
The Scholarship Committee shall comprise the following members:
1. Registrar- Chairman
2. Deans of respective School- Member
3. Controller of Examination- Member
4. Director ERP- Member
5. Chief Account & Finance officer- Member
6. Director Admission- Member Secretary
Functions and Responsibilities
• Formulate, review, and update scholarship guidelines and procedures in alignment
with university objectives and statutory requirements.
• Screen and evaluate scholarship applications based on predefined eligibility criteria,
academic merit, socio-economic background, and other relevant parameters.
• Recommend deserving students for various types of scholarships such as merit-based,
need-based, special-category, and externally funded schemes.
• Verify submitted documents for authenticity and completeness before final approval.
• Coordinate with the Finance Department for timely disbursement of scholarship funds
to selected students.
• Maintain comprehensive records of all scholarship applications, approvals, rejections,
and fund utilization.
• Address grievances or appeals related to scholarship allocation, if any, in a timely and
impartial manner.

• Prepare periodic reports and analytics on scholarship distribution for review by the
Academic Council or university management.
• Co-opt subject experts or departmental representatives, when necessary, to assist in the
evaluation process.
• The decisions made by the Scholarship Committee shall be final and binding in all
scholarship-related matters.

7. SCHOLARSHIP POLICY DISCLAIMER
• This is the official Scholarship Policy of Sanjivani University and serves as a general
framework outlining the university’s provisions, criteria, and procedures for awarding
scholarships.
• All scholarships offered by the university are subject to the eligibility criteria,
availability of funds, and the final approval of the Scholarship Committee and the
competent authority of Sanjivani University.
• The decisions made by the Scholarship Committee shall be final and binding on all
applicants. Sanjivani University reserves the right to revise, modify, suspend, or
withdraw any scholarship scheme or its provisions at any time without prior notice,
based on regulatory changes or institutional considerations.
• Any grievances or disputes related to scholarship applications, selection, or
disbursement shall be addressed solely through the University’s Scholarship
Committee, in accordance with the prevailing rules and regulations.

13 SEP 2024

**Subject: - Establishment of the Sanjivani University, Kopargaon, Gat No. 24 and 25, Kopargaon, Taluka Kopargaon, District- Ahmednagar- 423601, Maharashtra by the Government of Maharashtra-regarding**

Sir,

Kindly refer to the letter No. SRES/ESTT/2024/101 dated 26.07.2024 an e-mail dated 19.08.2024 on the subject cited above. The university has submitted attested copies of Maharashtra Private Universities (Establishment and Regulation) Act, 2023 (Mah. Act No. VIII of 2024) wherein the Sanjivani University, Kopargaon, Gat No. 24 and 25, Kopargaon, Taluka Kopargaon, District- Ahmednagar- 423601, Maharashtra established by the State Government of Maharashtra as a State (Private) University with effect from 15.02.2024 vide Notification No.Misc. 2023/C.R- 130/Uni.4 dated 15.02.2024 operational with effect from 18.04.2024 (Notification No.SFU-2023/C.R.127/Uni-4 dated 18.04.2024). Accordingly, the name of the university has been included in the list of universities, as maintained by UGC under Section 2(f) of UGC Act. 1956. The list is available on the UGC website www.ugc.gov.in.

The University may note and adhere to the following:

1. The inclusion of the name of the University in the list of universities maintained by the UGC does not by itself allow the University to make admissions in its programmes. This may be done only after creation of required academic and physical infrastructure facilities, including library, laboratories and appointment of teaching and supporting staff as per the norms and standards laid down by the UGC and Statutory Council(s) concerned.
2. The University is requested to submit detailed information (soft copy only) in the prescribed format (available at UGC website at the link https://www.ugc.gov.in/oldpdf/proformaprivateuniversities.pdf along with supporting documents duly attested by the Registrar of the University within a period of 3 months from the receipt of this letter. The University is also requested to upload the filled in format and appendices on its website by giving a link on the home page so that the information is accessible to the students and general public.

---

## Page 2

3. After receipt of the Information in the above-mentioned format, the following procedure would be adopted by the UGC for inspection of the University: -

a. The information received from the University in the prescribed format would be posted on the UGC website within 10 days of the receipt of the information. Comments would be invited from the general public within 1 month thereafter about the information submitted by the University. The comments received from the general public would be placed before the UGC Expert Committee for perusal.

b. An Expert Committee would be constituted by the UGC to ascertain whether the University fulfils the criteria in terms of programmes, faculty, infrastructural facilities, financial viability, etc. as laid down from time to time by the UGC and other concerned statutory bodies such as AICTE, BCI, NMC, DCI, INC, NCTE, PCI, etc.

c. The UGC Expert Committee would inspect the University within 3 months of the receipt of the information for assessment of infrastructure and other facilities available with the University. The report submitted by the UGC Expert Committee would be sent to the University within 2 weeks of the completion of the visit for comments.

d. The University may respond to the report within a period of two weeks after its receipt. In case no comments are received from the University within the timeframe, it would be presumed that the University has no comments to offer and further action would be taken on the report. After receipt of comments from the University, the report and comments received from the University, if any, would be placed before a Standing Committee of the UGC. The recommendations of the Standing Committee would be then placed before the Commission for consideration.

e. The Commission shall take final decision in the matter. It may require the University to submit further compliance report in respect of the observations/suggestions of the UGC Expert Committee/Standing Committee. The compliance report submitted by the University shall be placed before the Commission for consideration.

f. On approval of the Commission, a letter would be issued by the UGC to the effect that the University fulfills the criteria in terms of programmes, faculty, infrastructural facilities, financial viability, etc. as laid down from time to time by the UGC and other concerned statutory bodies.

g. If it is found that the Private University has, even after getting an opportunity to do so, failed to comply with the provisions of the various UGC Regulations including UGC (Establishment of and maintenance of standards in Private Universities) Regulations, 2003 and suggestions given by the UGC Expert Committee, the Commission may pass an order prohibiting the University from offering any course for the award of the degree and/or the post-graduate degree/diploma, as the case may be, till the deficiency is rectified.

h. The UGC may also take necessary action against a University awarding a first degree and /or a post-graduate degree, which are not specified by the UGC and inform the public in general through a public notification.

4. As per the judgment of the Hon'ble Supreme Court in the case of Prof. Yashpal Vs. State of Chhattisgarh, there is no provision to have Regional Centres/off-campus centres beyond the territorial jurisdiction of the State. In view of the judgement of Hon'ble apex court, the University is requested to adhere to the following:-

---

## Page 3

13 SEP 2004

a. No off-campus centre(s) is opened by the University outside the territorial jurisdiction of the State in view of the judgement of Hon'ble Supreme Court of India in case of Prof. Yashpal Vs. State of Chhattisgarh
b. In case the University has already started any off campus centre outside the State, it must be closed down immediately. It may also be ensured that any off campus centre within the State shall be opened only as per the provision laid down in the UGC (Establishment of and maintenance of standards in Private Universities) Regulations, 2003 and with the prior approval of UGC.
c. The University shall not establish/open/affiliate/recognise any off campus or constituent college/unit, outside the main campus of university, without the prior and specific approval of UGC.
d. It may also be ensured that any off campus centre within the State shall be opened only as per the provision laid down in the UGC (Establishment of and maintenance of standards in Private Universities) Regulations, 2003 and with the prior approval of UGC.
e. The University shall not have any affiliated Colleges.
f. The University must ensure that there is no franchising of higher education even for the purpose of distance education.
g. The University has to follow University Grants Commission (Minimum Standards and Procedures for Award of Ph.D.Degree) Regulations, 2022, as amended from time to time.
5. No Open and Distance Learning programmes and on-line programmes shall be started without the prior approval of UGC. Relevant UGC Regulations are available on the UGC website www.ugc.gov.in.
6. The University is empowered to award degrees as specified under Section 22 of the UGC Act through its main campus in regular mode with the approval of Statutory Bodies of the University and Statutory Council(s), wherever required. However, universities can award only such degrees as specified by UGC. The University is required to follow the UGC Notification on Specification of Degrees 2014 and its amendments issued from time to time wherein consolidated list of UGC approved nomenclature of degrees has been specified under Section 22 of the UGC Act, 1956 wherein entry level of each degree and duration of programme have been provided. The approved nomenclature may be followed by the specific area of specialization to be reflected in the parentheses. This notification is available on the UGC website www.ugc.ac.in under the link https://www.ugc.gov.in/pdfnews/1061840_specification-of-degrees-july-2014.pdf.
7. The university shall immediately appoint an Ombudsperson in accordance with UGC (Redressal of Grievances of Students) Regulations, 2023 for redressal of grievances of students preferably within two months of starting academic activities. The details of Ombudsperson should be displayed on the website of university and send to UGC also.
8. The University shall follow UGC Guidelines on Public Self-Disclosure by Higher Education Institutions which are available on UGC website at the link https://www.ugc.gov.in/pdfnews/7822003 GUIDELINES-ON-PUBLIC-SELF-DISCLOSURE-BY-HIGHER-EDUCATION-INSTITUTIONS.pdf.

---

## Page 4

13 SEP 2004

9. The University is required to follow the UGC (Establishment of and Maintenance of Standards in Private Universities) Regulation, 2003 and other Regulations issued from time to time and posted on UGC website.

This is issued with the approval of Competent Authority.

Vice-Chancellor’s Message

Vice-Chancellor’s Message

Dr. AjayKumar G. Thakur

Vice-Chancellor's Desk

Vice-Chancellor's Desk

Sanjivani University is an educational landscape imperative to remain steadfast in its pursuit of global academic excellence. Our motto, "Strategize, Succeed, and Shine," encapsulates our commitment to empowering individuals to thrive academically and beyond.

In alignment with the ethos of our institution, we are dedicated to implementing the principles outlined in the New Education Policy-NEP 2020 to provide graduates empowered with multidisciplinary outcomes. At Sanjivani University, we prioritize outcome-based learning, leveraging cutting-edge pedagogy and technology to foster holistic development. Our interdisciplinary curriculum, research-oriented programs, and hands-on learning experiences are designed to cultivate critical thinking, creativity, and ethical values. When an individual is provided with the appropriate resources and support, they can transcend their limitations and achieve remarkable success.

Sanjivani offers international language programs as a part of the curriculum as well as co-curricular learning. Learning another language will boost communication skills and open doors to exciting international opportunities.

Sanjivani University has something special to offer for everyone. The journey towards realizing one's full potential requires the right environment, guidance, and opportunities which is a core of the offerings of Sanjivani University.

Warm regards,

Dr. AjayKumar G. Thakur

Vice-Chancellor, Sanjivani University

Sanjivani University, Kopargaon,

(Maharashtra), 423601.

VISION AND MISSION

Vision And Mission

Vision And Mission

VISION

Our Vision

To emerge as a globally recognised university for excellence in Education, Research, Innovation and Entrepreneurship in order to produce effective leaders for serving the society.

MISSION

Our Mission

Providing state of the art infrastructure, industry centric curriculum and education focusing on ethics and critical thinking.

Promoting an innovative and research oriented environment for fostering effective problem solving and entrepreneurial development.

Collaborating with National and International Industries and Higher Education Institutions for academics and research.

Creating a conducive environment for a vibrant, happy and responsible society by contributing to Institutional Social Responsibility (ISR) initiatives.

Late Hon’ble Shankarraoji Kolhe Saheb

Former Minister,

Government of Maharashtra

SANJIVANI RURAL EDUCATION SOCIETY

The Pioneer of Socio-Economic Transformation

Championing Maharashtra's Rural Development, Late Shankarrao Genuji Kolhe Saheb (1929-2022) was a multifaceted personality who left a lasting mark. He commenced his career as an agriculturist by training and began his public service as a village headman (sarpanch). He later served multiple terms as a member of the Maharashtra Legislative Assembly, advocating for farmers' rights.

He famously led a farmers' march to the Reserve Bank of India demanding lower loan interest rates.

Saheb championed policies to improve the lives of rural communities. He also played a key role in establishing educational institutions and cooperative industries, promoting social and economic development in rural areas.

Saheb's legacy extends beyond politics. He was a strong proponent of education and established twelve educational campuses under his institute. His dedication to social upliftment continues to inspire many.

Sanjivani University, Kopargaon,

Near Shirdi, Ahilyanagar

(Maharashtra), 423601.

सबका माʁलक एक
|| ॐ ±ी साɠनाथाय नमः ||

Late Shankarrao Genuji Kolhe Saheb (1929-2022) was a
dynamic force for Maharashtra's rural development, leaving an
indelible legacy in agriculture, public service, and community
upliftment. Beginning his journey as an agriculturist by training,
Saheb initially entered public service as a village headman
(Sarpanch), eventually serving multiple terms as a member of the
Maharashtra Legislative Assembly and as a Minister in the State
Government. Throughout his career, he tirelessly advocated for
farmers’ rights and championed policies aimed at improving the
quality of life in rural communities.

Beyond politics, Saheb was a visionary in education and
economic development. He was instrumental in founding numerous
Educational Institutions and cooperative industries, driving both
social and economic progress across rural Maharashtra. Under his
leadership, twelve educational campuses ﬂourished, reﬂecting his
deep commitment to education as a tool for empowerment. Saheb’s
dedication to social progress and rural advancement continues to
inspire countless individuals, ensuring his legacy lives on.

In the pursuit of excellence in education and global
collaboration, Sanjivani University, under the leadership of its
dedicated chairman, is committed to bringing quality international
education to its students' doorsteps. Through strategic partnerships
with leading Institutions worldwide, the University oﬀers diverse
programs and opportunities for students to engage with
International faculty, exchange ideas with peers from diﬀerent
cultures, and gain valuable insights into global issues. Beyond the
classroom, Sanjivani University provides state-of-the- art
facilities and hands-on learning experiences to ensure holistic
development and prepare students for success in today's
competitive landscape. With a focus on equipping students with
the knowledge, skills, and mind set needed to excel in their chosen
ﬁelds, Sanjivani University is dedicated to building a brighter
future for its community and the world at large.
Message From The Chairman’s Desk
Late Shankarraoji Kolhe Saheb
Former Minister,
Government of Maharashtra
Hon. Shri. Nitinrao Shankarrao Kolhe
Chairman
Sanjivani Rural Education Society
The Pioneer of Socio-Economic Transformation
FOUNDER

Sanjivani University takes pride in providing a top-notch
education that prepares learners for success in the real world.
Sanjivani's academic fraternity is passionate about their subjects and
committed to helping learners reach their full potential. They
challenge learners to think critically and develop their curiosity. The
University understands the importance of a relevant education. It
designs its programs with inputs from industry experts, so learners
graduate with the skills employers seek. The Learners gain hands-on
experience through foreign internships and projects.

Sanjivani believes in comprehensive student development.
The University oﬀers career guidance programs to spark learners'
entrepreneurial spirit and research opportunities. Its goal is to foster
individuals who are ready to make a positive impact on society. Our
student exchange programs with top internationally reputed
Universities broaden their horizons and help them become globally
competent. In today's world scenario, understanding diﬀerent
cultures is a major factor in success, and at Sanjivani we provide
such an environment with holistic development for all its
stakeholders. At Sanjivani, Sustainability is at the heart of
everything we do.
Message From The President’s Desk
Hon'ble Shri. Amit Nitinrao Kolhe
President
Sanjivani University

Sanjivani University prioritizes preparing learners for success in
today's dynamic job market. The University's industry-powered
curriculum is designed to meet the evolving needs of the industries
ensuring graduates possess the skills and knowledge required to excel in
their chosen ﬁelds, Collaborations with leading industry partners,
International internships, and hands-on projects provide learners with real-
world experiences that complement their academic learning and prepare
them for the professional world.

At Sanjivani University, academic excellence lies at the heart of its
mission. The University's dedicated faculty members are experts in their
respective ﬁelds, fostering a culture of excellence in teaching, research,
and entrepreneurship. The University’s faculty serves as mentors and
guides, encouraging students to think critically, follow their passions, and
reach their full academic potential. Through personalized academic
advising and customized learning plans, the University provides learners
with the essential tools to succeed beyond the classroom.

State-of-the-art laboratories, a dedicated Maker's Space, an
extensive library, and top-tier infrastructure, combined with 24*7 access to
blended learning, empower students to become skilled professionals and
highly sought after in their ﬁelds.

As Vice Chancellor, I, Dr. A. G. Thakur, encourage all learners to
fully embrace the wealth of opportunities available at Sanjivani University.
Whether your ambitions lie in advanced studies, launching your own
ventures, or creating a positive impact within your community, Sanjivani
University is committed to supporting and guiding you every step of the
way toward achieving your goals.
Message From The Vice Chancellor’s Desk
Dr. A. G. Thakur
Vice Chancellor
Sanjivani University
About Sanjivani University

Founded in 1983, the Sanjivani Group of Institutes is a renowned educational organization oﬀering
programs in engineering, management, pharmacy, polytechnic, and more. Known for academic excellence and
holistic development, it stands among Maharashtra's top institutions.

An extension of this legacy, Sanjivani University combines innovation with interdisciplinary education.
Through industry-driven programs, skill development, and a modern curriculum, it prepares students for global
challenges, driven by a mission to leverage technology and science for socio-economic growth.

Life at Sanjivani
At Sanjivani University, the pursuit of wisdom is constant which guides
the students to carve out successful and meaningful life for themselves
and make world a better place

Sports Facilities
Sanjivani University has built a World Class Sports Infrastructure which is beneﬁtting our
students in building desired level of competency and gaining fitness in their favourite sports. It
will enable them to qualify for the National & International Level Sporting Events as well as
winning laurels for their Institutions and the Country. Our State of the art gymnasium is well
equipped with the Latest equipment along with the steam bath facility. Our Sports
Infrastructure is well equipped to train the students and harness their latent potential in the
wide ranging sports. Our sports activities are being conducted under the able guidance of the
highly qualiﬁed instructors as well as National and International Reputed Sportsman.

1) Eligibility for UG and PG Programmes under School of Engineering and Technology
2) Eligibility for UG and PG Programs under School of Commerce and Management
a) Admissions into B.B.A

Higher Secondary School Certiﬁcate (10+2) or its equivalent examination with 40% marks
from any stream. (35% for Reserved category and 40% for Open category candidate as per
Maharashtra state government rules) and English as one of the Subjects.

Three-year Diploma Course (after S.S.C.) of Technical Education conducted by Government
of Maharashtra or its equivalent.

Two-year Diploma Course in Pharmacy (after H.S.C.) of Board of Technical Education
conducted by Government of Maharashtra or its equivalent.
Admission Eligibility
Admission Eligibility
a) Admissions into First Year (B.Tech)

Passed 10+2 examination with Physics and Mathematics as compulsory subjects along with
one of the Chemistry/Biotechnology/Biology/Technical Vocational subjects, obtained at least 45%
marks (40% in case of candidate belonging to Reserved Category) in the above subjects taken
together.
AND

Non Zero Score in JEE Main Paper I

Valid Score in MHT-CET/PERA CET/SUET
b) Admissions into Direct Second Year (DSY B.Tech. Lateral Entry)

Candidates passing SSC or HSC and also Diploma in Engineering or Technology or Bachelor of
Science or Diploma of Vocation (D. Voc.) or its equivalent from a recognized Institution.
c) Admissions into Post Graduation (M.Tech)

Bachelor's degree or equivalent in the relevant ﬁeld, obtained at least 50% marks (45% in case
of candidate belonging to reserved category) at the qualifying Examination.
AND

Obtained score in GATE examinations.

Valid Score in PERA CET/SUET.

Admission Eligibility
Higher Secondary School Certiﬁcate (10+2) with Vocational (MCVC)-Minimum Competency based
vocational course Examination with 45% marks (40% for reserved category candidate as per
Maharashtra state Government rules).
b) Admissions into M.B.A.

Recognized Bachelor's Degree of minimum 3-4 years duration.

Obtained at least 50% marks (45% in case of candidate belonging to reserved category) at
the qualifying Examination.
AND
Obtained score in CAT/MAT/XAT/ATMA/AIMA/MAH-MBA-CET examinations
Valid Score in PERA CET/SUET.
c) Admissions into B. Com.

Passed 10+2 or equivalent course in any discipline from any recognized Board / Council /
University with minimum 45% marks for general (open) category and 40% marks for students of
reserve class category.
d) Admissions into M.Com.
Recognized Bachelor's Degree of minimum 3 years duration in relevant discipline. Obtained at least
40% marks at the qualifying Examination.

ABOUT US

Sanjivani University

Sanjivani University, Kopargaon, is a premier institution of higher education committed to excellence in teaching, research, and innovation.

Established under the visionary leadership of the Sanjivani Group of Institutes, the University was founded with the objective of creating a transformative learning environment that nurtures intellectual growth, professional competence, and social responsibility.

Offering a comprehensive range of undergraduate, postgraduate, and doctoral programs across Engineering & Technology, Management, Commerce, Sciences, Pharmacy, and interdisciplinary domains, Sanjivani University ensures academic depth combined with practical relevance.

The University follows a learner-centric and outcome-based education model aligned with the National Education Policy (NEP) 2020, emphasizing experiential learning.

Life at Sanjivani

“Sanjivani University, situated in the peaceful surroundings of Taluka Kopargaon, invites you. Our campus provides a calm place for academic pursuits because of its beautiful surroundings and a tremendous amount of nature. Situated in the glory of nature, Sanjivani University maintains a balance between accessibility and peace. Here, in the center of campus activity, students experience a dynamic academic environment bolstered by state-of-the-art facilities and a supportive community. Every student at Sanjivani University is guaranteed an enriching and gratifying experience, regardless of whether they choose to take up demanding academic endeavors or grasp the many chances for personal development.”

Welcome to

Sanjivani University

Sanjivani University was established on the 40th anniversary of the Sanjivani Rural Education Society

The Legacy of

Learning at Sanjivani University

Sanjivani’s legacy is defined by excellence, innovation, and global cooperation. Prioritizing academic rigor and research, it fosters a culture of continuous learning. By forging partnerships with renowned universities worldwide, Sanjivani enhances the academic journey for students and facilitates cutting-edge research opportunities for faculty. Yet, its most profound impact lies in its community of scholars, students, and alumni united in driving positive change. Together, they propel Sanjivani as a beacon of knowledge, innovation, and social impact, ensuring a lasting legacy for generations to come.

Fortinet Centre of Excellence at Sanjivani University

Fortinet, a global leader in cybersecurity solutions, has partnered with Sanjivani University to establish the Fortinet Centre of Excellence on August 28, 2024. This milestone initiative is set to redefine cybersecurity education by providing students with industry-relevant training, real-world project experience, and advanced resources..

IBM Centre of Excellence at Sanjivani University

Sanjivani University proudly inaugurated the IBM Centre of Excellence (CoE) on January 23, 2025. This milestone reflects our commitment to fostering cutting-edge technological skills, industry readiness, and innovation among our students.…
